@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspaceDraftPilotMaterializationTests.cs

    private static PushBackDesign Assemble(PushBackPilotComposition composition)
    {
        Assert.True(composition.IsComposed, composition.Reason);
        return new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(PilotKit.Catalog).BuildDesign(composition.Editor!, composition.Inputs!);
    }

    private static string WriteFrom(PushBackPilotDraftState initial, PushBackPilotDraftState current) =>
        PilotKit.Persist(Assemble(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog)));

    /// <summary>
    /// A canonical variant of a fixture: the state it materializes to, edited through the editor API, assembled by the product
    /// and put back in the same envelope. Its text is what the product itself would write for that content.
    /// </summary>
    private static string Variant(string fixture, Action<PushBackPilotDraftState> edit)
    {
        var state = PilotKit.State(fixture);
        edit(state);
        var design = new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(PilotKit.Catalog).BuildDesign(state.Editor, state.Inputs);
        return Rewrite(PilotKit.Text(fixture), embed => embed.Design = new RackProjectStore().Serialize(RackProject.ForPushBack(design)));
    }

@@@@ NEW
@@@@ END

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspaceDraftPilotMaterializationTests.cs

    [Fact]
    public void AnExceptionInsideTheBaseOfComposeForWriteIsARejectionWithTheExceptionPrefix()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        PushBackPilotComposition? composition = null;

        var exception = Record.Exception(() => composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, ThrowingCatalog()));

        Assert.Null(exception);
        Assert.NotNull(composition);
        Assert.False(composition!.IsComposed);
        Assert.Null(composition.Editor);
        Assert.Null(composition.Inputs);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotMaterializer.MaterializeFailurePrefix, composition.Reason, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnExceptionInsideComposeForWriteItselfIsARejectionWithTheComposePrefix()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        current.Editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[0].Cells[0] = null!;
        PushBackPilotComposition? composition = null;

        var exception = Record.Exception(() => composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog));

        Assert.Null(exception);
        Assert.NotNull(composition);
        Assert.False(composition!.IsComposed);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeFailurePrefix, composition.Reason, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspaceDraftPilotMaterializationTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    [Fact]
    public void ComposeForWriteIsPureAndMutatesNeitherState()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-02-modulos");
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-02-modulos");
        current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.75;
        var initialCopy = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(initial);
        var currentCopy = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(current);
        var baseline = current.Editor.SideA.WorkingBaseline;
        var session = initial.Editor.SideA.ModuleSession;

        var first = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog);
        Assemble(first);
        var second = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog);

        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(initialCopy, initial));
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(currentCopy, current));
        Assert.Same(baseline, current.Editor.SideA.WorkingBaseline);
        Assert.Same(session, initial.Editor.SideA.ModuleSession);
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.Text("pb-02-modulos"), initial.Origin);
        Assert.NotSame(initial.Editor, first.Editor);
        Assert.NotSame(current.Editor, first.Editor);
        Assert.NotSame(current.Inputs, first.Inputs);
        Assert.NotSame(first.Editor, second.Editor);
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.Persist(Assemble(first)), PilotKit.Persist(Assemble(second)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ComposeForWriteIgnoresTheOriginOfTheCurrentState()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.5;
        var foreign = new PushBackPilotDraftState(current.Editor, current.Inputs, "not an envelope at all");

        var withRealOrigin = WriteFrom(initial, current);
        var withForeignOrigin = WriteFrom(initial, foreign);

        Assert.Equal(withRealOrigin, withForeignOrigin);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ComposeForWriteIsTotalAndRejectsABaseThatNoLongerMaterializes()
    {
        var good = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var cases = new List<PushBackPilotComposition>
        {
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(null!, good, PilotKit.Catalog),
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(good, null!, PilotKit.Catalog),
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(good, good, null!),
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(new PushBackPilotDraftState(good.Editor, good.Inputs, "garbage"), good, PilotKit.Catalog),
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(new PushBackPilotDraftState(good.Editor, good.Inputs, PilotKit.Text("pb-20-ab")), good, PilotKit.Catalog),
            PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(new PushBackPilotDraftState(good.Editor, good.Inputs, PilotKit.Text("pb-21-cola")), good, PilotKit.Catalog),
        };

        Assert.All(cases, composition =>
        {
            Assert.False(composition.IsComposed);
            Assert.Null(composition.Editor);
            Assert.Null(composition.Inputs);
            Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(composition.Reason));
        });
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ComposeForWriteTakesWhatIsEditedFromTheDraftAndWhatIsNotFromTheCurrentBase()
    {
        // The initial state is the base of V2 (custom header); the current state is V1 with an edited scale.
        var v2 = PilotKit.Text("pb-31-v2-modulos");
        var initial = PilotKit.Admitted(v2);
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;

        var written = WriteFrom(initial, current);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { ScalePath + ": 1.5 -> 2.25" }, PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), written));
        Assert.NotEqual(PilotKit.DesignOf(PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base")), written);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACleanDraftComposesExactlyItsBase()
    {
        foreach (var fixture in PilotKit.Canonical)
        {
            var text = PilotKit.Text(fixture);
            var registry = new DraftRegistry();
            var draft = registry.Begin(
                DraftKit.S1, "R1", DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), PilotKit.Admitted(text)),
                Comparer, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy).Draft!;

            Assert.False(draft.IsDirty);
            Assert.Equal(PilotKit.DesignOf(text), WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState));
        }
    }

    private static DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> BeginOn(string text) =>
        new DraftRegistry().Begin(
            DraftKit.S1, "R1", DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), PilotKit.Admitted(text)),
            Comparer, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy).Draft!;

    [Fact]
    public void ComposeForWriteRejectsAnyDifferenceOtherThanTheScaleAndNeverRevertsOrWritesItInSilence()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var edits = new Dictionary<string, Action<PushBackPilotDraftState>>
        {
            ["PalletTolerance"] = state => state.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 1.0,
            ["DimensionStyle"] = state => state.Inputs.Annotations.DimensionStyle = "SIMPLEX",
            ["PalletHeightOfACell"] = state => state.Editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[0].Cells[0].PalletHeight += 2.0,
            ["FrontCount"] = state => state.Editor.SideA.SetFrontCount(2),
            ["RearTopeSaque"] = state => state.Editor.SideA.RearTopeSaque += 1.5,
            ["PostCatalogId"] = state => state.Inputs.PostCatalogId = "POSTE-OTRO",
        };

        foreach (var (name, edit) in edits)
        {
            var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            edit(current);
            Assert.False(Comparer.Equals(initial, current), name);

            var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog);

            Assert.False(composition.IsComposed, name);
            Assert.Null(composition.Editor);
            Assert.Null(composition.Inputs);
            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.NonPilotContentReason, composition.Reason);
        }

        // The scale together with something else is still a rejection; the scale alone is not.
        var both = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        both.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 3.0;
        both.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 1.0;
        Assert.False(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, both, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed);
        var onlyScale = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        onlyScale.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 3.0;
        Assert.True(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, onlyScale, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnInPlaceEditOfTheCurrentEditorOutsideThePilotMakesComposeForWriteReject()
    {
        var draft = BeginOn(PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base"));
        Assert.True(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed);

        draft.CurrentDraftState.Editor.SideA.SetFrontCount(draft.CurrentDraftState.Editor.SideA.Structure.Count + 1);

        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, PilotKit.Catalog);
        Assert.False(composition.IsComposed);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.NonPilotContentReason, composition.Reason);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepPilotEditsOnIsPureAndGivesTheNewBaseWithTheScaleOfTheDraft()
    {
        var newInitial = PilotKit.State("pb-02-modulos");
        newInitial.Inputs.PalletTolerance = 7.25;
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        current.Inputs.PalletTolerance = 9.0;
        var newInitialBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(newInitial);
        var currentBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(current);

        var kept = PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(newInitial, current);

        Assert.NotSame(newInitial, kept);
        Assert.NotSame(current, kept);
        Assert.NotSame(newInitial.Editor, kept.Editor);
        Assert.NotSame(newInitial.Inputs, kept.Inputs);
        Assert.NotSame(current.Inputs, kept.Inputs);
        Assert.Equal(newInitial.Origin, kept.Origin);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, kept.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal(7.25, kept.Inputs.PalletTolerance);
        Assert.Equal(2.0, newInitial.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(newInitialBefore, newInitial));
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(currentBefore, current));
        Assert.Equal(2.25, current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

        var withTheBaseScale = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(newInitial);
        withTheBaseScale.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(withTheBaseScale, kept));
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(null!, current));
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(newInitial, null!));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepMineAfterAnExternalModuleChangeWritesTheNewModulesAndTheScaleOfTheDraftAndReturnsToCleanWhenTheScaleIsRestored()
    {
        var v1 = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var v2 = PilotKit.Text("pb-31-v2-modulos");
        var draft = BeginOn(v1);

        // 1. V1 is open and the scale is edited.
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);

        // 2. An external V2 changes ONLY the module layer (a custom header), with the same scale as V1.
        var second = PilotKit.Admitted(v2);
        Assert.Equal(1.5, second.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(PilotKit.Admitted(v1), second));
        Assert.NotEqual(PilotKit.DesignOf(v1), PilotKit.DesignOf(v2));

        // 3. The user keeps the draft on the current version: the initial state moves to V2, and the surface sets as the
        // current state the new base with the scale of the draft.
        var keep = draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
            DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), second), confirmed: true);
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.Reloaded, keep.Status);
        Assert.Equal(v2, draft.InitialDraftState.Origin);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(DraftEditStatus.Updated, draft.SetCurrentDraftState(PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(second, draft.CurrentDraftState)).Status);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

        // 4. The composition carries the header of V2 and the scale of the draft.
        var composed = WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ScalePath + ": 1.5 -> 2.25" }, PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), composed));
        Assert.Contains(PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v1), composed), difference => difference.StartsWith("$.PushBack.Structure.Modules[2]", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        // 5. Giving the scale back its original value leaves the draft Clean and the composition identical to V2.
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 1.5;
        Assert.False(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void KeepMineAfterAnExternalChangeOfContentThePilotDoesNotEditNeverRevertsItAndComposesTheNewContentPlusTheScaleOfTheDraft()
    {
        var v1 = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var v2 = Variant("pb-01-base", state =>
        {
            state.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 2.0;
            state.Editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[0].Cells[0].PalletHeight += 3.0;
            state.Inputs.Annotations.DimensionStyle = "SIMPLEX";
        });

        // V2 is a design of its own and changes EDITABLE content the pilot does not edit.
        var changed = PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v1), PilotKit.DesignOf(v2));
        Assert.Contains("$.PushBack.Structure.PalletTolerance: 4 -> 6", changed);
        Assert.Contains("$.PushBack.Structure.DimensionStyle: null -> \"SIMPLEX\"", changed);
        Assert.Contains(changed, difference => difference.StartsWith("$.PushBack.Structure.Fronts[0].Levels[0].PalletHeight: ", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        var second = PilotKit.Admitted(v2);
        Assert.False(Comparer.Equals(PilotKit.Admitted(v1), second));
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), WriteFrom(second, second));

        // 1. V1 is open and the scale is edited; 2. the user keeps the draft on V2, with no rebase of the current state.
        var draft = BeginOn(v1);
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        var keep = draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
            DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), second), confirmed: true);
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.Reloaded, keep.Status);

        // 3. Composing now would silently revert V2 to V1: it is a typed rejection.
        var rejected = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, PilotKit.Catalog);
        Assert.False(rejected.IsComposed);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.NonPilotContentReason, rejected.Reason);

        // 4. The surface rebases the current state with KeepPilotEditsOn: V2 plus the scale of the draft.
        draft.SetCurrentDraftState(PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(second, draft.CurrentDraftState));
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        var composed = WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ScalePath + ": 1.5 -> 2.25" }, PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), composed));

        // 5. Giving the scale back leaves the draft Clean and the composition identical to V2.
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 1.5;
        Assert.False(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState));
    }

    private static string RoundTrip(string text, double? editedScale)
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.Admitted(text);
        var current = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(initial);
        if (editedScale.HasValue) current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = editedScale.Value;
        return WriteFrom(initial, current);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver1_TheRejectionComparesTheCurrentStateAsItIsAgainstACopyOfTheInitialOneWithItsScaleNeutralizedAndMutatesNeitherState()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var edits = new Dictionary<string, Action<PushBackPilotDraftState>>
        {
            ["PalletTolerance"] = state => state.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 1.0,
            ["PostPeralte"] = state => state.Inputs.PostPeralte += 0.5,
            ["RearTopeSaque"] = state => state.Editor.SideA.RearTopeSaque += 1.5,
            ["PalletHeightOfACell"] = state => state.Editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[0].Cells[0].PalletHeight += 2.0,
        };

        foreach (var (name, edit) in edits)
        {
            // The edit is in the current state, together with a valid and different scale: never written in silence.
            var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 3.25;
            edit(current);
            var initialBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(initial);
            var currentBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(current);

            var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog);

            Assert.False(composition.IsComposed, name);
            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.NonPilotContentReason, composition.Reason);
            Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(initialBefore, initial), name);
            Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(currentBefore, current), name);
            Assert.Equal(3.25, current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
            Assert.Equal(1.5, initial.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

            // The same edit in the INITIAL state only is a difference too, seen from the other side.
            var editedInitial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            edit(editedInitial);
            var untouched = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            untouched.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 3.25;
            Assert.False(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(editedInitial, untouched, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed, name);
        }

        // The scale alone, in either direction, is the one difference that is composed.
        foreach (var scale in new[] { 0.25, 3.25, 1.5 })
        {
            var onlyScale = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            onlyScale.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = scale;
            Assert.True(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, onlyScale, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed, scale.ToString());
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver3_ABaseThatMaterializedAgainIsNotTheInitialStateIsRejectedBeforeComposingAndANormalBaseStillComposes()
    {
        // The initial and the current state carry the SAME edit of content that the pilot does not edit, as if the base had been
        // materialized with another catalog than the one that materializes it now: the origin gives another state, and nothing is composed.
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        initial.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 1.0;
        var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        current.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 1.0;
        current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.5;
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(initial, PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(current, initial)));
        var initialBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(initial);
        var currentBefore = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(current);

        var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog);

        Assert.False(composition.IsComposed);
        Assert.Null(composition.Editor);
        Assert.Null(composition.Inputs);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.BaseChangedReason, composition.Reason);
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(initialBefore, initial));
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(currentBefore, current));

        var normal = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        var edited = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
        edited.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.5;
        Assert.True(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(normal, edited, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver6_AScaleThatIsNotAFiniteNumberGreaterThanZeroIsRejectedAndNeverComposedAndTheInfiniteOneIsADeclaredExtension()
    {
        var initial = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");

        foreach (var scale in new[] { double.NaN, double.PositiveInfinity, double.NegativeInfinity, 0.0, -0.0, -1.0, -1e-9 })
        {
            var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = scale;
            PushBackPilotComposition? composition = null;

            var thrown = Record.Exception(() => composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog));

            Assert.Null(thrown);
            Assert.False(composition!.IsComposed, scale.ToString());
            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializer.InvalidScaleReason, composition.Reason);
            Assert.Null(composition.Editor);
            Assert.Null(composition.Inputs);
        }

        foreach (var scale in new[] { 1e-9, 0.25, 12.5, double.MaxValue })
        {
            var current = PilotKit.State("pb-01-base");
            current.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = scale;

            Assert.True(PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(initial, current, PilotKit.Catalog).IsComposed, scale.ToString());
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver2_KeepAfterAnExternalModuleChangeIsOneAtomicStepThatComposesTheNewModulesAndTheScaleOfTheDraft()
    {
        var v1 = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var v2 = PilotKit.Text("pb-31-v2-modulos");
        var draft = BeginOn(v1);
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        draft.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "typed");
        var second = PilotKit.Admitted(v2);

        // ONE call: the initial state moves to V2 and the current state is V2 plus the scale of the draft; there is no step in between.
        var keep = draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
            DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), second), PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn, confirmed: true);

        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.Reloaded, keep.Status);
        Assert.Equal(v2, draft.InitialDraftState.Origin);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal("typed", draft.PendingText["AnnotationScale"]);
        draft.RemovePendingText("AnnotationScale");
        var composed = WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ScalePath + ": 1.5 -> 2.25" }, PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), composed));
        Assert.Contains(PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v1), composed), difference => difference.StartsWith("$.PushBack.Structure.Modules[2]", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 1.5;
        Assert.False(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver2_KeepAfterAnExternalChangeOfContentThePilotDoesNotEditNeverLeavesTheStepThatWouldRevertItAndComposesAtOnce()
    {
        var v1 = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var v2 = Variant("pb-01-base", state =>
        {
            state.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 2.0;
            state.Editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[0].Cells[0].PalletHeight += 3.0;
            state.Inputs.Annotations.DimensionStyle = "SIMPLEX";
        });
        var second = PilotKit.Admitted(v2);
        var draft = BeginOn(v1);
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;

        var keep = draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
            DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), second), PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn, confirmed: true);

        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.Reloaded, keep.Status);
        // With the two-parameter version this is the moment of a typed rejection, until the surface rebases the current state; here the
        // very same call already did it, so composing now carries the new content plus the scale.
        var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, PilotKit.Catalog);
        Assert.True(composition.IsComposed, composition.Reason);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ScalePath + ": 1.5 -> 2.25" }, PilotKit.JsonDiff(PilotKit.DesignOf(v2), WriteFrom(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState)));
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(second, PilotKit.State("pb-01-base")), PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(second, second)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CarryOver2_AKeepOnThatFailsLeavesTheDraftWithoutTheIntermediateStateAndThePendingTextStays()
    {
        var v1 = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var second = PilotKit.Admitted(PilotKit.Text("pb-31-v2-modulos"));
        var draft = BeginOn(v1);
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
        draft.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "typed");
        var baseBefore = draft.StaleBase;
        var originBefore = draft.InitialDraftState.Origin;

        var thrown = Record.Exception(() => draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
            DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), second),
            (initial, current) => throw new InvalidOperationException("keep-on-boom"),
            confirmed: true));

        Assert.Equal("keep-on-boom", thrown?.Message);
        Assert.Same(baseBefore, draft.StaleBase);
        Assert.Equal(originBefore, draft.InitialDraftState.Origin);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal("typed", draft.PendingText["AnnotationScale"]);
    }

/// <summary>A state with EVERY classified field populated, so a census can exercise each one. Built by the editor's own API.</summary>
internal static class PilotRich
{
    public static PushBackPilotDraftState Build()
    {
        var editor = new PushBackCompositeEditorState();
        editor.SideA.LoadNew();
        editor.SideA.SetFrontCount(2);
        editor.SideA.AdjustLevels(0, 1);
        editor.SideA.RearTopeSaque = 4.25;
        editor.SideA.RearTopePieceId = "TOPE-A";
        editor.SideA.DefensePieceId = "DEFENSA-A";
        editor.SideA.Cell(0, 1).RearTopeEnabled = false;
        editor.SideA.Cell(1, 0).PalletsDeepOverride = 3;
        editor.SideA.Cell(1, 0).DrawPallet = true;
        editor.SideA.Cell(1, 0).HighEndBeamPeralte = 4.0;
        editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[1].Bfr = 12.5;
        editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[1].BeamLength = 90.5;
        editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[1].Cells[0].BeamLengthOverride = 72.0;
        editor.SideA.Structure.Fronts[1].Cells[0].ClearHeight = 7.5;
        editor.SideA.ToggleCell(1, 0, extendSelection: false);

        editor.SetSideBPresent(true);
        editor.SideB.LoadNew();
        editor.SetSlotCount(2);
        editor.SetSlotPresent(PushBackSide.B, 0, true);
        editor.SetSlotPresent(PushBackSide.B, 1, true);
        editor.SideB.RearTopePieceId = "TOPE-B";
        editor.SideB.DefensePieceId = "DEFENSA-B";
        editor.SetGap(54.5);
        editor.SetCentralSeparator(true);
        editor.SetStructureOverride(PushBackSide.A, 5);
        editor.SetStructureOverride(PushBackSide.B, 6);
        editor.SetDefaults(PushBackCellTopology.Encontradas, PushBackRunDirection.BToA);
        editor.SetCell(0, 0, PushBackCellTopology.Corrida, PushBackRunDirection.AToB);
        editor.SetCorridaDepth(0, 1, 3);
        editor.SetActiveSelection(PushBackSideSelection.Both);
        editor.SideA.ToggleCell(0, 1, extendSelection: true);
        editor.SetActiveSide(PushBackSide.B);

        // The module authority, populated: baseline, session, commit, signature, reconciliation and a dormant tail.
        var catalog = PilotKit.Catalog;
        var source = PilotKit.State("pb-02-modulos");
        editor.SideA.AdoptLoadedBaseline(new PushBackResolver(catalog).Resolve(
            new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(catalog).BuildDesign(source.Editor, source.Inputs)));
        _ = editor.SideA.ModuleSession;
        editor.SideA.CommitModuleEdits();
        editor.SideA.LastModuleReconciliation = (RackModuleReconciliationResult)RuntimeHelpers.GetUninitializedObject(typeof(RackModuleReconciliationResult));
        editor.ParkDormantTail(
            new[] { new DynamicRackModuleDesign { ModuleId = "B:M2", Length = 40.0 } },
            new[] { new DynamicHeaderLineOverride { PostIndex = 1, ModuleId = "B:M6" } });
        editor.MarkDormantTailConsumed();

        var inputs = new PushBackEditorInputs
        {
            Pallet = new PalletSpecification(40.0, 46.0, 58.0, 900.5, "lb"),
            PalletsDeep = 5,
            PostCatalogId = "POSTE-X",
            PostPeralte = 3.25,
            PalletTolerance = 3.5,
            FirstLevelDatum = 1,
            BeamDepth = 5.5,
            Annotations = new DynamicAnnotationOptions
            {
                NumberFronts = true, NumberLevels = true, DrawRackName = true, AnnotationScale = 1.75,
                Dimensions = DimensionDetail.Standard, DimensionViews = DimensionViewVisibility.Frontal | DimensionViewVisibility.Planta, DimensionStyle = "STYLE-1",
            },
            ManualHeaderHeightOverride = 150.5,
            DerivedPostReinforced = false,
            DerivedPostReinforcementHeight = 60.5,
            DerivedPostHeight = 140.5,
            SeparatorCountOverride = 3,
            SeparatorSpacingOverride = 18.5,
        };
        inputs.SafetySelections.Add(Selection("A"));
        inputs.SafetySelections.Add(Selection("B"));
        return new PushBackPilotDraftState(editor, inputs, "origin-text-A");
    }

    private static SelectiveSafetySelection Selection(string tag)
    {
        var selection = new SelectiveSafetySelection
        {
            ElementId = "SAFETY-" + tag,
            Quantity = 2,
            Side = SafetySide.Left,
            LowEndOnly = true,
            AuthoredSide = SafetySide.Right,
            NearFace = SafetyFacePiece.Of("NEAR-" + tag),
            FarFace = SafetyFacePiece.Of("FAR-" + tag),
            BothEndsAreLoadFaces = true,
            DesviadorCellsAreByPost = true,
            BootSidesDeclared = true,
            Tope = new SelectiveTopeConfig { Shared = false, Fondo = 1, Saque = 2.5, Frontal = true },
            Desviador = new SelectiveDesviadorConfig { Longitud = 5.5, PrimerNivelAltura = 3.5 },
            Parrilla = new SelectiveParrillaConfig { Frontal = false, Lateral = false, Frente = 4.5, Cantidad = 2 },
        };
        selection.SecondLoadFacePosts.Add(1);
        selection.PostSides.Add(new SafetyPostSide { PostIndex = 1, Side = SafetySide.Both });
        selection.DerivedAisles.Add(new DerivedAisleEntry { PostIndex = 2, Authored = SafetySide.Left });
        selection.Tope.OffCells.Add(new SelectiveGridCell { Frente = 1, Level = 2 });
        selection.Desviador.OffCells.Add(new SelectiveGridCell { Frente = 2, Level = 1 });
        selection.Guia.OffCells.Add(new SelectiveGridCell { Frente = 0, Level = 1 });
        selection.Parrilla.OffCells.Add(new SelectiveGridCell { Frente = 3, Level = 0 });
        selection.Defensa.Posts.Add(new SafetyPostDefense { PostIndex = 1, ExitLength = 12.5, EntranceLength = 36.5, ExitAuto = true, EntranceAuto = true });
        selection.Bota.PieceId = "BOTA-" + tag;
        selection.Bota.Placement = BootPlacement.Rear;
        selection.Bota.Automatic = BootPlacement.Both;
        selection.Bota.Posts.Add(new BootPostPlacement { PostIndex = 1, Placement = BootPlacement.EntryExit });

        selection.BotaB.PieceId = "BOTA-B-" + tag;

        selection.BotaB.Automatic = BootPlacement.Rear;
        selection.BotaB.Posts.Add(new BootPostPlacement { PostIndex = 2, Placement = BootPlacement.Rear });
        selection.BotaB.BlankPosts.Add(4);
        return selection;
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheAdapterRunsInsideTheTwoPhaseCommitOfTheDraftCore()
    {
        var registry = new DraftRegistry();
        var read = DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(DraftKit.DefaultBase(), PilotKit.State("pb-01-base"));
        var draft = registry.Begin(DraftKit.S1, "R1", read, Comparer, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy).Draft!;
        draft.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "2.25");
        draft.SetPendingText("Other", "1");

        var rejected = draft.CrossBoundary(DraftBoundary.TabChange, new PushBackPilotFieldAdapter());
        Assert.Equal(DraftBoundaryDecision.Aborted, rejected.Decision);
        Assert.Equal("Other", Assert.Single(rejected.InvalidFields).FieldKey);
        Assert.Equal(1.5, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

        draft.RemovePendingText("Other");
        var current = draft.CurrentDraftState;
        var accepted = draft.CrossBoundary(DraftBoundary.TabChange, new PushBackPilotFieldAdapter());

        Assert.Equal(DraftBoundaryDecision.Proceeded, accepted.Decision);
        Assert.Same(current, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal(1.5, draft.InitialDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Dirty, draft.Status);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryEditableFieldIsExercisedByTheRichStateAndMutatingItMakesTheStatesDifferent()
    {
        var rich = PilotRich.Build();
        var baseline = PilotRich.Build();
        Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(baseline, rich));

        var editable = PilotCensus.Table.Where(pair => pair.Value == PilotFieldClass.Editable).Select(pair => pair.Key).ToHashSet(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var exercised = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        var failures = new List<string>();
        foreach (var (owner, field) in PilotCensus.EditableSlots(rich).ToList())
        {
            var key = PilotCensus.KeyOf(field);
            if (!Mutator.TryMutate(owner, field, out var undo)) continue;
            exercised.Add(key);
            if (Comparer.Equals(baseline, rich) || Comparer.Equals(rich, baseline)) failures.Add("mutation not detected: " + key);
            undo();
            if (!Comparer.Equals(baseline, rich)) failures.Add("undo did not restore equality: " + key);
        }

        // A reference to a part that holds more fields is exercised through those fields; every other field must be mutated itself.
        var containers = editable.Where(key => PilotCensus.Table.ContainsKey(key) && IsContainer(key)).ToList();
        var missing = editable.Except(exercised).Except(containers).OrderBy(key => key, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToList();
        Assert.True(failures.Count == 0, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, failures));
        Assert.True(missing.Count == 0, "editable fields the rich state does not exercise: " + string.Join(", ", missing));
    }

    private static bool IsContainer(string key) => Mutator.ContainerKeys.Contains(key);

    [Fact]
    public void MutatingEverySelectionFieldTheModuleAuthorityAndTheOriginLeavesTheStatesEqual()
    {
        var rich = PilotRich.Build();
        var baseline = PilotRich.Build();
        var checkedKeys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);

        foreach (var key in PilotCensus.Table.Where(pair => pair.Value != PilotFieldClass.Editable).Select(pair => pair.Key))
        {
            var slot = PilotCensus.FindSlot(rich, key);
            Assert.True(slot.HasValue, "the rich state has no slot for " + key);
            var (owner, field) = slot!.Value;
            Assert.True(Mutator.TryMutate(owner, field, out var undo), "cannot mutate " + key);
            checkedKeys.Add(key);

            Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(baseline, rich), "the comparator saw the change of " + key);
            Assert.True(Comparer.Equals(rich, baseline), "the comparator saw the change of " + key);
            undo();
        }

        Assert.Equal(PilotCensus.Table.Count(pair => pair.Value != PilotFieldClass.Editable), checkedKeys.Count);
    }

            if (field.DeclaringType == typeof(SafetyFacePiece)) continue;

            undo();

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspaceDraftPilotStateTests.cs
@@@@ OLD

/// <summary>Mutates one classified field of an instance in a way the comparator must (or must not) notice, and undoes it.</summary>
internal static class Mutator
{
    /// <summary>Fields that only hold a part whose own fields are exercised one by one.</summary>
    public static readonly HashSet<string> ContainerKeys = new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
    {
        "PushBackPilotDraftState.Editor", "PushBackPilotDraftState.Inputs",
        "PushBackCompositeEditorState.SideA", "PushBackCompositeEditorState.SideB",
        "PushBackEditorState.structure",
        "PushBackEditorInputs.Pallet", "PushBackEditorInputs.Annotations",
        "SelectiveSafetySelection.BotaB", "SelectiveSafetySelection.tope", "SelectiveSafetySelection.desviador",
        "SelectiveSafetySelection.defensa", "SelectiveSafetySelection.guia", "SelectiveSafetySelection.parrilla",
        "SelectiveSafetySelection.bota",
    };

    public static bool TryMutate(object owner, FieldInfo field, out Action undo)
    {
        undo = () => { };
        if (ContainerKeys.Contains(PilotCensus.KeyOf(field))) return false;

        var type = field.FieldType;
        var current = field.GetValue(owner);
        if (current is IEnumerable and not string || (type.IsGenericType && type.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(HashSet<>)))
        {
            return MutateCollection(current!, out undo);
        }

        object? replacement = Replacement(type, current);
        if (replacement is NoReplacement) return false;
        field.SetValue(owner, replacement);
        undo = () => field.SetValue(owner, current);
        return true;
    }

    private sealed class NoReplacement
    {
    }

    private static object? Replacement(Type type, object? current)
    {
        var underlying = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(type);
        if (underlying != null)
        {
            return current == null ? Replacement(underlying, null) : null;
        }

        if (type == typeof(bool)) return !(bool)(current ?? false);
        if (type == typeof(int)) return (int)(current ?? 0) + 1;
        if (type == typeof(double)) return double.IsNaN((double)(current ?? 0.0)) ? 2.0 : (double)(current ?? 0.0) + 1.5;
        if (type == typeof(string)) return ((string?)current ?? string.Empty) + "~";
        if (type.IsEnum)
        {
            var values = Enum.GetValues(type).Cast<object>().ToList();
            return values.First(value => !value.Equals(current));
        }

        if (type == typeof(SafetyFacePiece)) return SafetyFacePiece.Of(((SafetyFacePiece?)current)?.ElementId + "~");
        if (current == null && !type.IsValueType) return RuntimeHelpers.GetUninitializedObject(type);
        if (!type.IsValueType) return null;
        return new NoReplacement();
    }

    private static bool MutateCollection(object collection, out Action undo)
    {
        var type = collection.GetType();
        var elementType = type.IsGenericType ? type.GetGenericArguments()[0] : typeof(object);
        var element = NewElement(elementType);
        var add = type.GetMethod("Add", new[] { elementType })!;
        var remove = type.GetMethod("Remove", new[] { elementType });
        add.Invoke(collection, new[] { element });
        undo = () =>
        {
            if (collection is IList list && list.Count > 0)
            {
                list.RemoveAt(list.Count - 1);
            }
            else
            {
                remove!.Invoke(collection, new[] { element });
            }
        };
        return true;
    }

    private static object NewElement(Type type)
    {
        if (type == typeof(int)) return 77;
        if (type == typeof(double)) return 77.5;
        if (type == typeof(string)) return "x";
        if (type == typeof(ValueTuple<int, int>)) return (88, 99);
        return Activator.CreateInstance(type, nonPublic: true)!;
    }
}

/// <summary>I-64 F4-T3A: a comparer of the pilot state that can be told to throw (the draft then cannot decide whether it is dirty).</summary>
internal sealed class PilotEditThrowingComparer : IEqualityComparer<PushBackPilotDraftState>
{
    public bool Throw { get; set; } = true;

    public bool Equals(PushBackPilotDraftState? x, PushBackPilotDraftState? y)
    {
        if (Throw) throw new InvalidOperationException("the comparer threw");
        return PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(x, y);
    }

    public int GetHashCode(PushBackPilotDraftState obj) => PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.GetHashCode(obj);
}

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotEditHostTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotEditHostFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void TheOperationsOfADocumentNeverTouchTheDraftsOfAnotherAndABlockingMutationOfAnotherDocumentIsRejected()
    {
        var env = new Env();
        var a = env.Session;
        var draftA = env.Open(RackA, a);
        env.Type("2", RackA, a);
        var b = env.Hr.Activate(200);
        var draftB = env.Open(RackA, b);
        env.Type("8x", RackA, b);
        env.Hr.Activate(100);

        // With A active, the operations that change something and name B are refused and change nothing.
        var typed = env.Type("77", RackA, b);
        var committed = env.Commit(RackA, b);
        var crossed = env.Cross(PilotBoundaryKind.TabChange, RackA, b);
        var discarded = env.Port.DiscardRack(env.Origin(b), RackA);
        var all = env.Port.DiscardDocument(env.Origin(b));

        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Rejected, typed.Status);
        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Rejected, committed.Status);
        Assert.Equal(PilotBoundaryDecision.Aborted, crossed.Decision);
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(crossed.Reason));
        Assert.Equal(EditDiscardStatus.Rejected, discarded.Status);
        Assert.Equal(EditDiscardStatus.Rejected, all.Status);
        Assert.Equal("8x", draftB.PendingText[Key]);
        Assert.True(draftB.IsRegistered);
        Assert.Equal("2", draftA.PendingText[Key]);

        // And the operations of A change A alone.
        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Applied, env.Type("3", RackA, a).Status);
        Assert.Equal("8x", draftB.PendingText[Key]);
        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Applied, env.Commit(RackA, a).Status);
        Assert.Equal(3.0, Env.ScaleOf(draftA));
        Assert.Equal(Env.InitialScale, Env.ScaleOf(draftB));
    }

    public void GetFieldReportsEveryStateOfTheFieldAndWhatCanBeEdited()
    {
        var env = new Env();
        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldState.NotOpen, env.Field().State);
        Assert.False(env.Field().Exists);
        Assert.False(env.Field().CanEdit);

        var clean = env.Open(RackA);
        var view = env.Field();
        Assert.Equal((true, PilotFieldState.Clean, false, false, true), (view.Exists, view.State, view.IsPossiblyStale, view.IsProtected, view.CanEdit));
        Assert.Equal(WorkspacePilotValueText.Show(Env.InitialScale, out _), view.CommittedText);
        Assert.Null(view.PendingText);
        Assert.Null(view.PendingMessage);

        env.Type("abc");
        var dirty = env.Field();
        Assert.Equal((PilotFieldState.Dirty, true, true), (dirty.State, dirty.IsProtected, dirty.CanEdit));
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotFieldAdapter.NotANumberMessage, dirty.PendingMessage);
        Assert.Equal("abc", dirty.PendingText);

        var conflict = env.Open(RackB);
        Assert.True(conflict.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        var applying = env.Field(RackB);
        Assert.Equal((PilotFieldState.Applying, false), (applying.State, applying.CanEdit));
        conflict.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.NotAppliedConflict(new[] { new DraftDifference("A1", DraftDifferenceKind.EnvelopeText) }));
        var conflicted = env.Field(RackB);
        Assert.Equal((PilotFieldState.Conflict, true), (conflicted.State, conflicted.CanEdit));

        var unknown = env.Open("R7");
        Assert.True(unknown.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        unknown.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.CommitThrew("the write threw"));
        var unknownView = env.Field("R7");
        Assert.Equal((PilotFieldState.Unknown, false), (unknownView.State, unknownView.CanEdit));

        var error = env.Open("R8");
        error.EnterError("boom");
        var errorView = env.Field("R8");
        Assert.Equal((PilotFieldState.Error, false), (errorView.State, errorView.CanEdit));

        Assert.True(clean.IsRegistered);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotEditHostFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void AfterAnySequenceOfAcceptedAndRejectedEditsTheFieldHoldsTheLastAcceptedText()
    {
        var env = new Env();
        var draft = env.Open();
        var lastAccepted = (string?)null;

        foreach (var text in new[] { "1", "12", "12x", "", "  7 ", "9,5" })
        {
            Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Applied, env.Type(text).Status);
            lastAccepted = text;
            Assert.Equal(lastAccepted, env.Field().PendingText);
        }

        // The field stops being editable: the edits are refused and the last accepted text stays.
        draft.EnterError("boom");
        foreach (var text in new[] { "100", "abc" })
        {
            Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.NotEditable, env.Type(text).Status);
            Assert.Equal(lastAccepted, env.Field().PendingText);
        }

        // A request of another session is refused too.
        env.Hr.Activate(200);
        Assert.Equal(PilotFieldStatus.Rejected, env.Type("5", RackA, env.Session).Status);
        Assert.Equal(lastAccepted, env.Field(RackA, env.Session).PendingText);
        Assert.Equal(lastAccepted, draft.PendingText[Key]);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<double> TextCorpus()
    {
        yield return 1;
        yield return 2.25;
        yield return 1.23456;
        yield return 0.1;
        yield return 1E-05;
        yield return 1E+15;
        yield return 1E+20;
        yield return 123456789.125;
        yield return 100;
        yield return double.Epsilon;
        yield return double.MaxValue;
        var random = new Random(20261113);
        var count = 0;
        while (count < 400)
        {
            var value = BitConverter.Int64BitsToDouble(random.NextInt64());
            if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsInfinity(value) || value <= 0.0) continue;
            count++;
            yield return value;
        }
    }

    [Theory(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotEditHostFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    [InlineData("es-MX")]
    [InlineData("en-US")]
    [InlineData("de-DE")]
    [InlineData("")]
    public void TheShownTextOfAFiniteValueGreaterThanZeroIsReadBackAsTheSameDoubleAndPassesTheValidationInEveryCulture(string culture)
    {
        InCulture(culture, () =>
        {
            var separator = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat.NumberDecimalSeparator;
            var adapter = new PushBackPilotFieldAdapter();
            foreach (var value in TextCorpus())
            {
                var text = WorkspacePilotValueText.Show(value, out var reason);

                Assert.NotNull(text);
                Assert.Null(reason);
                Assert.True(LocalizedNumberParser.TryDouble(text!, out var back), "unreadable: " + text);
                Assert.Equal(BitConverter.DoubleToInt64Bits(value), BitConverter.DoubleToInt64Bits(back));
                Assert.True(adapter.Validate(Key, text!).IsValid, "invalid: " + text);
                Assert.DoesNotContain("E", text!, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                Assert.DoesNotContain("+", text!, StringComparison.Ordinal);
                Assert.DoesNotContain("-", text!, StringComparison.Ordinal);
                Assert.True(text!.Split(separator).Length <= 2);
                Assert.False(text.StartsWith(separator, StringComparison.Ordinal));
                Assert.False(text.EndsWith(separator, StringComparison.Ordinal));
                Assert.True(text.Length == 1 || text[0] != '0' || text.StartsWith("0" + separator, StringComparison.Ordinal));
            }
        });
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotEditHostTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    public void TheNoticeOfAVetoExpiresWhenTheSessionIsDiscardedWhenTheNextAdviceAllowsAndWhenTheSessionIsRetired()
    {
        // DiscardRack that leaves the session without risk.
        var env = new Env();
        env.Open();
        env.Type("abc");
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Veto, env.Close());
        env.Port.DiscardRack(env.Origin(), RackA);
        Assert.Empty(env.Port.TakeCloseNotices());

        // DiscardRack that leaves another rack at risk keeps the notice.
        env = new Env();
        env.Open(RackA);
        env.Open(RackB);
        env.Type("abc", RackA);
        env.Type("def", RackB);
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Veto, env.Close());
        env.Port.DiscardRack(env.Origin(), RackA);
        Assert.Single(env.Port.TakeCloseNotices());

        // DiscardDocument.
        env = new Env();
        env.Open();
        env.Type("abc");
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Veto, env.Close());
        env.Port.DiscardDocument(env.Origin());
        Assert.Empty(env.Port.TakeCloseNotices());

        // The next advice gives Allow because the pending text went away by other means.
        env = new Env();
        var draft = env.Open();
        env.Type("abc");
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Veto, env.Close());
        draft.RemovePendingText(Key);
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Allow, env.Close());
        Assert.Empty(env.Port.TakeCloseNotices());

        // The session is retired: its notice goes and the loss notice takes over.
        env = new Env();
        env.Open();
        env.Type("abc");
        Assert.Equal(CloseDecision.Veto, env.Close());
        env.Destroy();
        Assert.Empty(env.Port.TakeCloseNotices());
        Assert.Single(env.Port.TakeLossNotices());
    }

        var env = new Env();

        var error = env.Open("R8");
        Assert.Equal(DraftEditStatus.Updated, error.SetPendingText(Key, "abc").Status);
        error.EnterError("boom");

        // A draft is never applied with pending text (TryBeginApply refuses it) and a draft that cannot be edited takes none, so an unknown
        // or applying draft has no pending text and its crossing proceeds with nothing to validate; only a draft that went into error with
        // pending text can be blocked-and-not-editable at once, and for that one the crossing keeps.
        var unknown = env.Open("R7");
        Assert.True(unknown.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        unknown.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.CommitThrew("the write threw"));

        var applying = env.Open(RackB);
        Assert.True(applying.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);

        foreach (var (rack, state, pending, decision) in new[]
                 {
                     ("R8", PilotFieldState.Error, "abc", PilotBoundaryDecision.Kept),
                     ("R7", PilotFieldState.Unknown, (string?)null, PilotBoundaryDecision.Proceeded),
                     (RackB, PilotFieldState.Applying, (string?)null, PilotBoundaryDecision.Proceeded),
                 })
        {
            Assert.False(env.Field(rack).CanEdit);
            Assert.Equal(state, env.Field(rack).State);

            foreach (var kind in new[] { PilotBoundaryKind.TabChange, PilotBoundaryKind.RackChange })
            {
                var crossed = env.Cross(kind, rack);

                Assert.Equal(decision, crossed.Decision);
                Assert.False(crossed.StateChanged);
                Assert.Empty(crossed.InvalidFields);
                Assert.Null(crossed.Reason);
                Assert.Equal(pending, env.Field(rack).PendingText);
            }
        }

        Assert.Empty(env.Hr.Faults);
    }
@@@@ NEW
@@@@ END

@@@@ OLD
    private static DraftStaleBase YetAnotherBase() => DraftKit.Base(DraftKit.Mutable("D1", text: "{\"v\":9}"));

    // What a draft holds that ConfirmBase must never change.
    private sealed record Held(
        DraftStaleBase Base,
        DraftForm Initial,
        DraftForm Current,
        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Pending,
        string? LastApplyCause,
        IReadOnlyList<string> Warnings,
        bool IsDirty,
        DraftUnknownOrigin? UnknownOrigin,
        string? ErrorReason);

    private static Held Hold(DraftSession<DraftForm> draft) => new(
        draft.StaleBase, draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, draft.PendingText, draft.LastApplyCause, draft.Warnings,
        draft.IsDirty, draft.UnknownOrigin, draft.ErrorReason);

    private static void AssertHeld(Held before, DraftSession<DraftForm> draft)
    {
        Assert.Same(before.Base, draft.StaleBase);
        Assert.Equal(before.Initial, draft.InitialDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(before.Current, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(before.Pending.ToArray(), draft.PendingText.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(before.LastApplyCause, draft.LastApplyCause);
        Assert.Equal(before.Warnings.ToArray(), draft.Warnings.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(before.IsDirty, draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(before.UnknownOrigin, draft.UnknownOrigin);
        Assert.Equal(before.ErrorReason, draft.ErrorReason);
    }

@@@@ END

    public void ConfirmBaseDoesNotApplyToADraftThatIsNotRegisteredApplyingUnknownOrInError()
    {
        var detached = DraftKit.Born();
        var registry = new DraftRegistry();
        var removed = DraftKit.Born(registry);
        registry.DiscardByUser(DraftKit.S1, "R1");
        var cases = new Dictionary<string, DraftSession<DraftForm>>
        {
            ["Applying"] = DraftKit.ToApplying(DraftKit.Dirty()),
            ["UnknownCommit"] = DraftKit.ToUnknownCommit(DraftKit.Dirty()),
            ["UnknownPost"] = DraftKit.ToUnknownPost(DraftKit.Dirty()),
            ["Error"] = DraftKit.ToError(DraftKit.Dirty()),
            ["Detached"] = removed,
        };
        Assert.False(removed.IsRegistered);
        Assert.True(detached.IsRegistered);

        foreach (var (name, draft) in cases)
        {
            var before = Hold(draft);
            var status = draft.Status;
            var stale = draft.IsPossiblyStale;

            foreach (var reread in new[] { draft.StaleBase, OtherBase() })
            {
                var result = draft.ConfirmBase(reread);

                Assert.Equal(DraftConfirmStatus.NotApplicable, result.Status);
                Assert.Equal(status, result.DraftStatus);
                Assert.Empty(result.Differences);
                Assert.Equal(status, draft.Status);
                Assert.Equal(stale, draft.IsPossiblyStale);
                Assert.Empty(draft.ConflictDifferences);
                AssertHeld(before, draft);
            }

            Assert.True(name.Length > 0);
        }
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void AMatchInACleanOrDirtyDraftOnlyClearsThePossiblyStaleMarkAndNeverCleansDirty()
    {
        foreach (var dirty in new[] { false, true })
        {
            var draft = dirty ? DraftKit.Dirty() : DraftKit.Born();
            if (dirty) draft.SetPendingText("name", "typed");
            draft.MarkPossiblyStale();
            var before = Hold(draft);
            var status = draft.Status;
            Assert.Equal(dirty ? DraftStatus.Dirty : DraftStatus.Clean, status);

            var result = draft.ConfirmBase(DraftKit.DefaultBase());

            Assert.Equal(DraftConfirmStatus.Confirmed, result.Status);
            Assert.Equal(status, result.DraftStatus);
            Assert.Empty(result.Differences);
            Assert.False(draft.IsPossiblyStale);
            Assert.Equal(status, draft.Status);
            Assert.Equal(dirty, draft.CurrentDraftState != draft.InitialDraftState);
            Assert.Equal(dirty, draft.HasPendingText);
            AssertHeld(before, draft);
        }
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ADifferenceInACleanOrDirtyDraftPutsItInConflictWithTheOrderedDifferencesAndChangesNothingElse()
    {
        foreach (var dirty in new[] { false, true })
        {
            var draft = dirty ? DraftKit.Dirty() : DraftKit.Born();
            if (dirty) draft.SetPendingText("width", "42");
            draft.MarkPossiblyStale();
            var before = Hold(draft);
            Assert.Equal(dirty ? DraftStatus.Dirty : DraftStatus.Clean, draft.Status);
            var expected = draft.StaleBase.CompareWith(OtherBase()).Differences.ToArray();
            Assert.True(expected.Length > 1);

            var result = draft.ConfirmBase(OtherBase());

            Assert.Equal(DraftConfirmStatus.Conflict, result.Status);
            Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, result.DraftStatus);
            Assert.Equal(expected, result.Differences.ToArray());
            Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, draft.Status);
            Assert.Equal(expected, draft.ConflictDifferences.ToArray());
            Assert.True(draft.IsPossiblyStale);
            Assert.Equal(dirty, draft.HasPendingText);
            AssertHeld(before, draft);
            // The differences come ordered by key (ordinal), then by kind.
            Assert.Equal(expected.OrderBy(d => d.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal).ThenBy(d => d.Kind).ToArray(), expected);
        }
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void AMatchInAConflictAnswersConfirmedAndTheDraftStaysInConflictWithAllItsData()
    {
        var draft = DraftKit.ToConflict(DraftKit.Dirty());
        draft.SetPendingText("name", "typed");
        draft.MarkPossiblyStale();
        var before = Hold(draft);
        var differences = draft.ConflictDifferences.ToArray();

        var result = draft.ConfirmBase(DraftKit.DefaultBase());

        Assert.Equal(DraftConfirmStatus.Confirmed, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, result.DraftStatus);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(differences, draft.ConflictDifferences.ToArray());
        Assert.True(draft.IsPossiblyStale);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
        AssertHeld(before, draft);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ADifferenceInAConflictReplacesTheDifferencesAndTheDraftStaysInConflict()
    {
        var draft = DraftKit.ToConflict(DraftKit.Dirty());
        var before = Hold(draft);
        var old = draft.ConflictDifferences.ToArray();
        var expected = draft.StaleBase.CompareWith(YetAnotherBase()).Differences.ToArray();
        Assert.NotEqual(old, expected);

        var result = draft.ConfirmBase(YetAnotherBase());

        Assert.Equal(DraftConfirmStatus.Conflict, result.Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, result.DraftStatus);
        Assert.Equal(expected, result.Differences.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(expected, draft.ConflictDifferences.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, draft.Status);
        AssertHeld(before, draft);
    }

    public void KeepWithANullKeepOnThrowsBeforeAnythingElseAndChangesNothing()
    {
        var draft = Keepable(conflict: true);
        var before = Hold(draft);

        var thrown = Assert.IsType<ArgumentNullException>(Record.Exception(() => draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), null!, confirmed: true)));
        var withoutRead = Assert.IsType<ArgumentNullException>(Record.Exception(() => draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(null!, null!, confirmed: false)));

        Assert.Equal("keepOn", thrown.ParamName);
        Assert.Equal("keepOn", withoutRead.ParamName);

        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, draft.Status);

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void KeepChecksNotAllowedThenNotConfirmedThenNotAdmittedInTheOrderOfTheOtherVersionAndNeverCallsKeepOnWithoutAdmission()
    {
        var calls = 0;
        Func<DraftForm, DraftForm, DraftForm> keepOn = (initial, current) =>
        {
            calls++;
            return current;
        };

        var clean = DraftKit.Born();
        var cleanBefore = Hold(clean);
        var dirty = DraftKit.Dirty();
        var dirtyBefore = Hold(dirty);
        var notAdmitted = DraftIntentRead<DraftForm>.NotAdmitted("not admitted");

        // 1. Not allowed: a clean draft, and one that is Applying, whatever the confirmation and the admission.
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotAllowed, clean.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), keepOn, confirmed: true).Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotAllowed, clean.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(notAdmitted, keepOn, confirmed: false).Status);
        var applying = DraftKit.ToApplying(DraftKit.Dirty());
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotAllowed, applying.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), keepOn, confirmed: true).Status);
        // 2. Not confirmed, before the admission is looked at.
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotConfirmed, dirty.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(notAdmitted, keepOn, confirmed: false).Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotConfirmed, dirty.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), keepOn, confirmed: false).Status);
        // 3. Not admitted: the answer carries the diagnostic.
        var refused = dirty.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(notAdmitted, keepOn, confirmed: true);
        Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.NotAdmitted, refused.Status);
        Assert.Equal("not admitted", refused.Admission.Diagnostic);

        Assert.Equal(0, calls);
        AssertHeld(cleanBefore, clean);
        AssertHeld(dirtyBefore, dirty);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
@@@@ NEW
@@@@ END

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotPrepareTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    public void AKeepOnThatThrowsOrACopyThatThrowsLeavesTheDraftExactlyAsItWas()
    {
        // The copier fails at its n-th call after it is armed: the two copies of the initial state and the copy of the substitute are
        // all made before anything is assigned.
        foreach (var failAt in new[] { 1, 2, 3 })
        {
            var armed = 0;
            var counting = false;
            DraftForm Copy(DraftForm form)
            {
                if (counting && ++armed == failAt) throw new InvalidOperationException("copy-boom");
                return form with { };
            }

            var registry = new DraftRegistry();
            var draft = registry.Begin(DraftKit.S1, "R1", DraftKit.Read(), DraftKit.Comparer, Copy).Draft!;
            draft.SetCurrentDraftState(draft.CurrentDraftState with { Name = "Edited" });
            DraftKit.ToConflict(draft);
            draft.SetPendingText("name", "typed");
            draft.MarkPossiblyStale();
            var before = Hold(draft);
            counting = true;

            var thrown = Record.Exception(() => draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), (initial, current) => current, confirmed: true));

            Assert.NotNull(thrown);
            Assert.Equal("copy-boom", thrown!.Message);
            counting = false;
            AssertHeld(before, draft);
            Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, draft.Status);
            Assert.True(draft.IsPossiblyStale);
            Assert.NotEmpty(draft.ConflictDifferences);
        }

        var plain = Keepable(conflict: true);
        var plainBefore = Hold(plain);
        var failure = Record.Exception(() => plain.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(NewRead(), (initial, current) => throw new InvalidOperationException("keep-boom"), confirmed: true));

        Assert.Equal("keep-boom", failure?.Message);
        AssertHeld(plainBefore, plain);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, plain.Status);
        Assert.NotEmpty(plain.ConflictDifferences);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void TheSnapshotIsBuiltOnlyByFromWithCopiesAndTheStatusBeforeAnAttemptWhileApplying()
    {
        var read = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan());
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

        var input = PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(draft);

        Assert.Equal(PrepareKit.Rack, input.RackId);
        Assert.Same(draft.StaleBase, input.StaleBase);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Dirty, input.ConsideredStatus);
        Assert.True(input.IsDirty);
        Assert.False(input.HasPendingText);
        Assert.NotSame(draft.CurrentDraftState, input.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, input.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.NotSame(draft.InitialDraftState, input.InitialDraftState);
        // Changing the copies changes nothing of the draft.
        input.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 9.0;
        input.InitialDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 9.0;
        Assert.Equal(2.25, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.NotEqual(9.0, draft.InitialDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

        draft.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "3");
        Assert.True(PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(draft).HasPendingText);
        draft.RemovePendingText("AnnotationScale");

        // Under Applying the considered status is the one before the attempt (N-7), not Applying.
        Assert.True(draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Applying, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Dirty, PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(draft).ConsideredStatus);

        var clean = PrepareKit.DraftOf(IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan()));
        Assert.True(clean.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(clean).ConsideredStatus);
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(null!));
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ADraftThatIsNotCleanOrDirtyIsNotApplicableWhateverTheReread()
    {
        var read = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan());
        var other = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan("pb-02-modulos"));

        foreach (var name in new[] { "Conflict", "UnknownCommit", "UnknownPost", "Error" })
        {
            var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
            PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);
            Assert.True(draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
            switch (name)
            {
                case "Conflict":
                    draft.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.NotAppliedConflict(new[] { new DraftDifference("A1", DraftDifferenceKind.EnvelopeText) }));
                    break;
                case "UnknownCommit":
                    draft.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.CommitThrew("c"));
                    break;
                case "UnknownPost":
                    draft.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.AppliedPostRereadFailed("p"));
                    break;
                default:
                    draft.CompleteApply(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState>.NotAppliedFailure("f"));
                    draft.EnterError("boom");
                    break;
            }

            Assert.NotEqual(DraftStatus.Dirty, draft.Status);
            Assert.NotEqual(DraftStatus.Clean, draft.Status);
            foreach (var reread in new[] { read, other })
            {
                var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, reread);

                Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.NotApplicable, result.Kind);
                Assert.Null(result.Plan);
                Assert.Null(result.Reason);
                Assert.Empty(result.Differences);
            }
        }
    }

    public void ADifferenceOfTheBaseIsAConflictBeforeTheAdmissionAndWithTheOrderedDifferences()
    {
        var read = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan());
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

        // Different and admitted.
        var changed = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan("pb-02-modulos"));
        Assert.True(changed.Read.IsAdmitted);
        // Different and NOT admitted: the conflict is still the answer, because the comparison goes first (INV-06).
        var notAdmitted = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "ZZ", IntentKit.Member("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal("pb-02-modulos"))));
        Assert.False(notAdmitted.Read.IsAdmitted);

        foreach (var reread in new[] { changed, notAdmitted })
        {
            var expected = draft.StaleBase.CompareWith(reread.StaleBase).Differences.ToArray();
            Assert.NotEmpty(expected);

            var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, reread);

            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Conflict, result.Kind);
            Assert.Equal(expected, result.Differences.ToArray());
            Assert.Null(result.Plan);
            Assert.Null(result.Reason);
        }
    }

            PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy).Draft!;

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void InvalidD_ABuilderThatCannotBuildTheOutgoingSystemIsInvalidWithTheTypedErrorAndNothingIsWritten()
    {
        var scan = PrepareKit.RealScan();
        var read = IntentKit.Read(scan);
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

        // A catalog that counts its touches and fails after the n-th one. The touches of the stages of PREPARE are measured with the
        // real planner: the composition (a) and the builder (d) both read the catalog, and the failure is armed to come in the builder.
        var armed = new CountingCatalog(PilotKit.Catalog, int.MaxValue);
        var ready = PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.Prepare(PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(draft), read, armed.Catalog);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, ready.Kind);
        var total = armed.Touches;
        Assert.True(total > 0);

        var failures = new List<PushBackPilotPrepareResult>();
        for (var allowed = 0; allowed < total; allowed++)
        {
            var failing = new CountingCatalog(PilotKit.Catalog, allowed);
            failures.Add(PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.Prepare(PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(draft), read, failing.Catalog));
        }

        // Every failure is a typed Invalid and never an exception; at least one of them is the failure of the builder (d).
        Assert.All(failures, f =>
        {
            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Invalid, f.Kind);
            Assert.Null(f.Plan);
            Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(f.Reason));
        });
        Assert.Contains(failures, f => f.Reason!.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.SystemFailurePrefix, StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains(failures, f => f.Reason!.Contains("catalog-boom", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Dirty, draft.Status);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void InvalidE_AMutableMemberThatIsNotAvailableInTheOutgoingSystemOrWhoseEnvelopeDoesNotDecodeIsInvalidAndNeverErasesAView()
    {
        var origin = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base");
        var originFrontal = PrepareKit.Frontal();
        var cases = new Dictionary<string, string>
        {
            // A lateral cut at a post that does not exist: a phantom that RACKEDITAR would erase and the pilot never does.
            ["phantom-lateral"] = PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.MissingPost),
            // The frontal view of side B in a rack that has only side A.
            ["side-b"] = PrepareKit.Frontal(section: 2),
            // A frontal view with the section of the fixtures (-1) is a corrupt descriptor: it is never coerced into another view.
            ["corrupt-descriptor"] = PilotKit.Text("pb-01-base"),
            ["section-out-of-range"] = PrepareKit.Frontal(section: 9),
        };

        foreach (var (name, text) in cases)
        {
            var members = PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", originFrontal), ("B2", text));
            var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(members, originFrontal);
            PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

            var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, PrepareKit.Reread(members));

            Assert.True(result.Kind == PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Invalid, name);
            Assert.Null(result.Plan);
            // M-5 (I-64 F4-T4B): a descriptor that does not decode has no address to ask about, so it has its own text and is never reported as a
            // view that is "no longer available".
            var corrupt = name is "corrupt-descriptor" or "section-out-of-range";
            Assert.StartsWith((corrupt ? PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.ViewAddressPrefix : PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.ViewUnavailablePrefix) + "B2", result.Reason, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            Assert.EndsWith(corrupt ? PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.ViewAddressSuffix : PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.ViewUnavailableSuffix, result.Reason, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }

        Assert.NotEqual(origin, originFrontal);
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotPrepareTests.cs
