    private static (DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> Draft, PushBackPilotIntentResult Read, PushBackPilotUpdatePlan Plan) ReadyPlan(
        Func<PushBackPilotIntentScan>? scan = null, double scale = 2.25)
    {
        scan ??= () => PrepareKit.RealScan();
        var read = IntentKit.Read(scan());
        Assert.True(read.Read.IsAdmitted);
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, scale);
        var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, IntentKit.Read(scan()));
        Assert.True(result.Kind == PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, result.Reason);
        Assert.NotNull(result.Plan);
        Assert.Null(result.Reason);
        Assert.Empty(result.Differences);
        return (draft, read, result.Plan!);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void TheReadyPlanHoldsTheRackTheOriginTheExpectedBaseTheScaleAndTheMembersByKeyInOrdinalOrder()
    {
        var (draft, read, plan) = ReadyPlan();
        var post = PrepareKit.ExistingPost();

        Assert.Equal(PrepareKit.Rack, plan.RackId);
        Assert.Equal("A1", plan.OriginKey);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2", "C3" }, plan.ExpectedBase.Select(e => e.Key).ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(draft.StaleBase.Entries.Values.OrderBy(e => e.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray(), plan.ExpectedBase.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(draft.StaleBase.VariableRegistryText, plan.ExpectedVariableRegistryText);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, plan.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2", "C3" }, plan.Members.Select(m => m.Key).ToArray());
        // The view and the section as RACKEDITAR writes them: frontal with the section of the envelope, lateral with the post, plan with -1.
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("frontal", 0), ("lateral", post), ("planta", -1) }, plan.Members.Select(m => (m.View, m.Section)).ToArray());
        Assert.True(draft.StaleBase.CompareWith(read.StaleBase).IsMatch);
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void TheOutgoingDesignAndEnvelopeOfEachViewComeFromTheRereadEnvelopeAndNeverFromTheDraft()
    {
        // The three views carry a name that is not the one of the fixture and an unknown JSON member that a newer build wrote, and the
        // envelope that goes out keeps both. The reader of the intention does not admit an envelope with unknown members, so the re-read is
        // built by hand over the same texts, exactly as a later read of the drawing would hand them over.
        var post = PrepareKit.ExistingPost();
        var a1 = WithExtra(PrepareKit.Frontal(name: "Name of the rack"), "x1");
        var members = PrepareKit.BaseOf(
            ("A1", a1),
            ("B2", WithExtra(PrepareKit.Lateral(post, name: "Name of the rack"), "x2")),
            ("C3", WithExtra(PrepareKit.Planta(name: "Name of the rack"), "x3")));
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(members, a1);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);
        var ready = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, PrepareKit.Reread(members));
        Assert.True(ready.Kind == PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, ready.Reason);
        var plan = ready.Plan!;
        var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, PilotKit.Catalog);
        var design = new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(PilotKit.Catalog).BuildDesign(composition.Editor!, composition.Inputs!);
        var store = new RackProjectStore();

        foreach (var member in plan.Members)
        {
            var source = new RackEmbedStore().Deserialize(members.Entries[member.Key].EnvelopeText!)!;
            var expectedDesign = store.Serialize(RackProject.ForPushBack(design).WithSourceMetadataFrom(store.Deserialize(source.Design)));
            var envelope = new RackEmbedStore().Deserialize(member.EnvelopeText)!;

            Assert.Equal(expectedDesign, member.DesignText);
            Assert.Equal(member.DesignText, envelope.Design);
            Assert.Equal(RackEmbedDocument.KindPushBack, envelope.Kind);
            Assert.Equal(source.Id, envelope.Id);
            // Name never comes from the draft: it is the name of the envelope that was re-read for that view.
            Assert.Equal(source.Name, envelope.Name);
            Assert.Equal("Name of the rack", envelope.Name);
            Assert.Equal(member.View, envelope.View);
            Assert.Equal(member.Section, envelope.Section);
            Assert.NotNull(envelope.ExtensionData);
            Assert.Contains(envelope.ExtensionData!.Keys, k => k.StartsWith("x", StringComparison.Ordinal));
            // The scale is the only thing the pilot changed in the design that goes out.
            Assert.Contains("2.25", member.DesignText, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        }

        Assert.Equal(plan.Members.Select(m => m.EnvelopeText).ToArray(), plan.PostExpectation.Envelopes.Select(e => e.Text).ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(plan.Members.Select(m => m.Key).ToArray(), plan.PostExpectation.Envelopes.Select(e => e.Key).ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(2.25, plan.PostExpectation.AnnotationScale);
    }

    private static string WithExtra(string text, string member) =>
        IntentKit.Rewrite(text, embed =>
            embed.ExtensionData = new Dictionary<string, System.Text.Json.JsonElement>
            {
                [member] = System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse("\"value of " + member + "\"").RootElement.Clone(),
            });

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ThePlanIsImmutableAndItsEqualityIsExplicitOrdinalElementByElementAndInOrder()
    {
        var (_, _, plan) = ReadyPlan();
        var (_, _, same) = ReadyPlan();
        var (_, _, otherScale) = ReadyPlan(scale: 3.5);

        Assert.NotSame(plan, same);
        Assert.True(plan.Equals(same));
        Assert.True(plan.Equals((object)same));
        Assert.Equal(plan.GetHashCode(), same.GetHashCode());
        Assert.True(plan.Members[0].Equals(same.Members[0]));
        Assert.True(plan.PostExpectation.Equals(same.PostExpectation));
        Assert.False(plan.Equals(otherScale));
        Assert.False(plan.Equals(null));
        Assert.False(plan.Equals((object)"plan"));

        // The collections cannot be changed through their interfaces, and the plan keeps only strings and values.
        Assert.True(((System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<PushBackPilotMemberPlan>)plan.Members).IsReadOnly);
        Assert.True(((System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<DraftStaleBaseEntry>)plan.ExpectedBase).IsReadOnly);
        Assert.True(((System.Collections.Generic.ICollection<PushBackPilotKeyedText>)plan.PostExpectation.Envelopes).IsReadOnly);
        foreach (var type in new[] { typeof(PushBackPilotUpdatePlan), typeof(PushBackPilotMemberPlan), typeof(PushBackPilotPostExpectation) })
        {
            Assert.All(type.GetProperties(), p => Assert.True(p.SetMethod == null, type.Name + "." + p.Name + " has a setter"));
            Assert.All(type.GetFields(System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Public | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic),
                f => Assert.True(f.IsInitOnly, type.Name + "." + f.Name + " is mutable"));
        }

        // Ordinal: the same text in another case is another text.
        var member = plan.Members[0];
        var lowered = new PushBackPilotMemberPlan(member.Key, member.View, member.Section, member.DesignText, member.EnvelopeText.ToLowerInvariant());
        Assert.False(member.Equals(lowered));
        Assert.True(member.Equals(new PushBackPilotMemberPlan(member.Key, member.View, member.Section, member.DesignText, member.EnvelopeText)));
        // In order: the same members in another order are another plan.
        var reversed = new PushBackPilotUpdatePlan(
            plan.RackId, plan.OriginKey, plan.ExpectedBase, plan.ExpectedVariableRegistryText, plan.AnnotationScale,
            plan.Members.Reverse(), plan.PostExpectation);
        Assert.False(plan.Equals(reversed));
        var sameAgain = new PushBackPilotUpdatePlan(
            plan.RackId, plan.OriginKey, plan.ExpectedBase, plan.ExpectedVariableRegistryText, plan.AnnotationScale,
            plan.Members, plan.PostExpectation);
        Assert.True(plan.Equals(sameAgain));
        // The scale is compared with Equals: NaN equals NaN and zero equals zero.
        Assert.True(new PushBackPilotPostExpectation(double.NaN, Array.Empty<PushBackPilotKeyedText>())
            .Equals(new PushBackPilotPostExpectation(double.NaN, Array.Empty<PushBackPilotKeyedText>())));
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ThePlanIsBuiltOnlyFromTheRereadSoAChangeOfTheCurrentScaleOnlyChangesTheScaleAndTheTextsThatCarryIt()
    {
        var (_, _, first) = ReadyPlan(scale: 2.25);
        var (_, _, second) = ReadyPlan(scale: 3.5);

        Assert.Equal(first.ExpectedBase.ToArray(), second.ExpectedBase.ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(first.Members.Select(m => (m.Key, m.View, m.Section)).ToArray(), second.Members.Select(m => (m.Key, m.View, m.Section)).ToArray());
        Assert.NotEqual(first.AnnotationScale, second.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.All(first.Members.Zip(second.Members), pair => Assert.NotEqual(pair.First.EnvelopeText, pair.Second.EnvelopeText));
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ThePilotNeverErasesAViewAPhantomThatWasAlreadyInTheDrawingMakesTheUpdateInvalidAndIsNeverInThePlan()
    {
        var origin = PrepareKit.Frontal();
        var withPhantom = PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", origin), ("B2", PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.MissingPost)));
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(withPhantom, origin);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

        var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, PrepareKit.Reread(withPhantom));

        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Invalid, result.Kind);
        Assert.Null(result.Plan);

        // The same rack without the phantom is Ready, and the plan redraws only the views that exist.
        var without = PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", origin), ("B2", PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost())));
        var clean = PrepareKit.DraftOf(without, origin);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(clean, 2.25);
        var ready = PrepareKit.Prepare(clean, PrepareKit.Reread(without));
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, ready.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2" }, ready.Plan!.Members.Select(m => m.Key).ToArray());
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void ReadOnlyAndNonMutableMembersOfTheRereadNeverEnterThePlan()
    {
        var origin = PrepareKit.Frontal();
        var lateral = PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost());
        var entries = new[]
        {
            new DraftStaleBaseEntry("A1", DraftMembership.Mutable, "frontal", "0", origin),
            new DraftStaleBaseEntry("B2", DraftMembership.Mutable, "lateral", PrepareKit.ExistingPost().ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), lateral),
            new DraftStaleBaseEntry("X9", DraftMembership.ReadOnly, "planta", "-1", PrepareKit.Planta()),
        };
        var members = DraftStaleBase.Create(entries);
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(members, origin);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);

        var result = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, PrepareKit.Reread(members));

        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, result.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2" }, result.Plan!.Members.Select(m => m.Key).ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2", "X9" }, result.Plan.ExpectedBase.Select(e => e.Key).ToArray());
    }

    private sealed record DraftHeld(

        PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(draft.InitialDraftState), PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(draft.CurrentDraftState),

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su copia en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void PrepareNeverChangesTheDraftWhateverItsOutcome()
    {
        var read = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan());
        var changed = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan("pb-02-modulos"));
        var notAdmitted = PrepareKit.Reread(read.StaleBase, notAdmitted: "x");
        var cases = new List<(string Name, DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> Draft, PushBackPilotIntentResult Reread)>();

        var ready = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(ready, 2.25);
        cases.Add(("ready", ready, read));

        var conflict = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(conflict, 2.25);
        cases.Add(("conflict", conflict, changed));

        var refused = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(refused, 2.25);
        cases.Add(("not admitted", refused, notAdmitted));

        var invalid = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(invalid, 0.0);
        cases.Add(("invalid", invalid, read));

        var clean = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        cases.Add(("nothing to apply", clean, read));

        var pending = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(pending, 2.25);
        pending.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "3");
        cases.Add(("pending text", pending, read));

        var stale = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(stale, 2.25);
        stale.MarkPossiblyStale();
        cases.Add(("possibly stale", stale, read));

        foreach (var (name, draft, reread) in cases)
        {
            var before = Hold(draft);
            var comparer = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance;

            PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, reread);
            PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, reread);

            var after = Hold(draft);
            Assert.True(before.Status == after.Status, name);
            Assert.Equal(before.IsDirty, after.IsDirty);
            Assert.Equal(before.IsPossiblyStale, after.IsPossiblyStale);
            Assert.Same(before.Base, after.Base);
            Assert.True(comparer.Equals(before.Initial, after.Initial), name);
            Assert.True(comparer.Equals(before.Current, after.Current), name);
            Assert.Equal(before.Pending, after.Pending);
            Assert.Equal(before.LastApplyCause, after.LastApplyCause);
            Assert.Equal(before.Warnings, after.Warnings);
            Assert.Equal(before.ConflictDifferences, after.ConflictDifferences);
            Assert.Equal(before.Scale, after.Scale);
        }
    }

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotPrepareFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void PrepareDoesNotPutTheDraftInConflictNorCleanDirtyNorTakeTheSlotOfTheUpdate()
    {
        var registry = new DraftRegistry();
        var read = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan());
        var begun = registry.Begin(DraftKit.S1, PrepareKit.Rack, read.Read, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy);
        var draft = begun.Draft!;
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);
        var changed = IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan("pb-02-modulos"));

        var conflict = PrepareKit.Prepare(draft, changed);

        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Conflict, conflict.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Dirty, draft.Status);
        Assert.Empty(draft.ConflictDifferences);
        Assert.False(registry.HasUpdateInProgress);
        Assert.True(draft.IsDirty);
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotReconciliationHostTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    // What the three operations must leave alone when they do not apply.
    // The base and the current state are held by reference. The initial state is a COPY by contract of the draft (N-2: every read of
    // InitialDraftState makes a new one), so its reference can never be the same: it is held as the copy that was made when it was
    // held and compared with the pilot's own comparer, which is the equality the draft itself uses to decide that it is dirty.
    private sealed record Held(
        DraftStaleBase Base,
        PushBackPilotDraftState Initial,
        PushBackPilotDraftState Current,
        string InitialOrigin,
        double Scale,
        IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Pending,
        DraftStatus Status,
        bool IsPossiblyStale,
        bool IsDirty);

    private static Held Hold(DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> draft) => new(
        draft.StaleBase,
        draft.InitialDraftState,
        draft.CurrentDraftState,
        draft.InitialDraftState.Origin,
        draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale,
        draft.PendingText.OrderBy(p => p.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray(),
        draft.Status,
        draft.IsPossiblyStale,
        draft.IsDirty);

    private static void AssertHeld(Held before, DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> draft)
    {
        Assert.Same(before.Base, draft.StaleBase);
        Assert.Same(before.Current, draft.CurrentDraftState);
        Assert.True(PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(before.Initial, draft.InitialDraftState), "the initial state of the draft changed");
        Assert.Equal(before.InitialOrigin, draft.InitialDraftState.Origin);
        Assert.Equal(before.Scale, draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal(before.Pending, draft.PendingText.OrderBy(p => p.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray());
        Assert.Equal(before.Status, draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(before.IsPossiblyStale, draft.IsPossiblyStale);
        Assert.Equal(before.IsDirty, draft.IsDirty);
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotReconciliationHostTests.cs

    [Fact(Skip = "I-64 F4B PB-T4b: sustituida por su equivalente escrito a mano en WorkspacePilotReconciliationHostFull (registro declarado superseding-characterization.md; decision del Owner owner-pbt4-decisions.md, pregunta 3).")]
    public void AnInvalidPendingTextDoesNotBlockTheThreeOperationsConfirmAndKeepLeaveItExactlyAsTypedAndDiscardEmptiesIt()
    {
        var confirmRig = new Rig();
        var confirmDraft = confirmRig.Open();
        Rig.Edit(confirmDraft);
        confirmDraft.SetPendingText("field", InvalidText);
        var before = Hold(confirmDraft);
        Assert.Equal(EditConfirmStatus.Confirmed, confirmRig.Core.TryConfirmPilotBase(confirmRig.Request()).Status);
        AssertHeld(before, confirmDraft);
        Assert.Equal(InvalidText, confirmDraft.PendingText["field"]);

        var keepRig = new Rig();
        var keepDraft = keepRig.Open();
        Rig.Edit(keepDraft);
        keepDraft.SetPendingText("field", InvalidText);
        keepDraft.MarkPossiblyStale();
        keepRig.Reader.Answer = rack => OriginChanged(rack, (ToleranceFrom, ToleranceTo));
        Assert.Equal(EditIntentStatus.Kept, keepRig.Core.TryKeepPilotEdit(keepRig.Request()).Status);
        Assert.Equal(InvalidText, keepDraft.PendingText["field"]);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, keepDraft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);

        var discardRig = new Rig();
        var discardDraft = discardRig.Open();
        Rig.Edit(discardDraft);
        discardDraft.SetPendingText("field", InvalidText);
        discardRig.Reader.Answer = rack => OriginChanged(rack, (ToleranceFrom, ToleranceTo));
        Assert.Equal(EditIntentStatus.Reloaded, discardRig.Core.TryDiscardAndReloadPilotEdit(discardRig.Request()).Status);
        Assert.False(discardDraft.HasPendingText);
    }

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.Tests/Workspace/WorkspacePilotUpdateGuardTests.cs
@@@@ OLD
    private static IEnumerable<string> ComposeForWriteViolations(string materializer)
    {
        var body = Src.BodyOf(materializer, @"public\s+static\s+PushBackPilotComposition\s+ComposeForWrite\s*\(");
        if (Regex.IsMatch(body, @"Copy\s*\(\s*current\s*\)")) yield return "ComposeForWrite copies the current state: it hides what the edit changed";
        if (Src.Count(body, @"PushBackPilotStateComparer\.Copy\s*\(\s*initial\s*\)") != 1) yield return "ComposeForWrite does not copy the initial state exactly once";
        if (!Regex.IsMatch(body, @"PushBackPilotStateComparer\.Instance\.Equals\s*\(\s*neutralInitial\s*,\s*current\s*\)")) yield return "ComposeForWrite does not compare the current state as it is";
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheCompositionForWritingComparesTheCurrentStateAsItIsAndNeverCopiesIt()
    {
        Assert.Empty(ComposeForWriteViolations(Src.Code(MaterializerPath)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheGuardOfTheCompositionTripsWhenTheCurrentStateIsCopiedBeforeTheComparison()
    {
        var code = Src.Code(MaterializerPath);

        Assert.NotEmpty(ComposeForWriteViolations(code.Replace(
            "PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(neutralInitial, current)",
            "PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(neutralInitial, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(current))",
            StringComparison.Ordinal)));
        Assert.NotEmpty(ComposeForWriteViolations(code.Replace("var neutralInitial = PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy(initial);", "var neutralInitial = initial;", StringComparison.Ordinal)));
        Assert.NotEmpty(ComposeForWriteViolations(code.Replace("PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(neutralInitial, current)", "PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance.Equals(current, neutralInitial)", StringComparison.Ordinal)));
    }
@@@@ NEW
@@@@ END

    [Fact]
    public void G4TripsOnACallerInTheControllerTheAdapterOrASecondCallInTheCore()
    {
        Assert.NotEmpty(G4Violations(Append(Product(), ControllerPath, "var u = _host.TryUpdatePilot(request, port);")));
        Assert.NotEmpty(G4Violations(Append(Product(), AdapterPath, "var u = core.TryUpdatePilot(request, port);")));
        Assert.NotEmpty(G4Violations(Append(Product(), HostRunPath, "var o = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(draft, request, port);")));
        Assert.NotEmpty(G4Violations(With(Product(), CorePath, code => code.Replace("public EditUpdateOutcome TryUpdatePilot(", "public EditUpdateOutcome Other() { return TryUpdatePilot(null!, null!); }\n        public EditUpdateOutcome TryUpdatePilot(", StringComparison.Ordinal))));
    }

    private static readonly string[] HappyCalls =
    {
        "Begin", "ReadForPrepare", "PrepareUnits", "BeginMutation", "Reread", "WriteUnit:A1", "WriteUnit:B2", "WriteUnit:C3", "Seal",
        "Dispose:mutation", "Post", "Dispose:scope",
    };

    private static InvalidOperationException Boom(string message) => new(message);

    private static string Cause(DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState> outcome) => outcome.Cause ?? string.Empty;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the check before the slot (step 1)

    [Fact]
    public void ThePrecheckOnlyStopsForTheThreeKindsThatNeedNeitherAReadNorACatalog()
    {
        var clean = PrepareKit.DraftOf(IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan()));
        var nothing = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Precheck(clean);
        Assert.False(nothing.Proceeds);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.NothingToApply, nothing.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.NothingToApplyReason, nothing.Reason);

        var pending = PrepareKit.DraftOf(IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan()));
        PrepareKit.SetScale(pending, 2.25);
        pending.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "3");
        var typed = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Precheck(pending);
        Assert.False(typed.Proceeds);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.PendingText, typed.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PendingTextReason, typed.Reason);

        var conflict = PrepareKit.DraftOf(IntentKit.Read(PrepareKit.RealScan()));
        PrepareKit.SetScale(conflict, 2.25);
        conflict.ConfirmBase(PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal("pb-02-modulos"))));
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Conflict, conflict.Status);
        var notApplicable = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Precheck(conflict);
        Assert.False(notApplicable.Proceeds);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotPrepareKind.NotApplicable, notApplicable.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.NotApplicableReason, notApplicable.Reason);

        // A dirty draft without pending text goes on: the missing read is not a reason to stop.
        var dirty = new PilotUpdateSetup().Draft;
        var go = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Precheck(dirty);
        Assert.True(go.Proceeds);
        Assert.Null(go.Reason);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the happy path: the order and what each step receives

    [Fact]
    public void TheRunFollowsTheOrderOfThePackageAndEndsAppliedWithTheRereadOfPost()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(HappyCalls, setup.Port.Calls.ToArray());
        Assert.True(outcome.Kind == DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Cause);
        Assert.NotNull(outcome.PostRead);
        Assert.True(outcome.PostRead!.IsAdmitted);
        Assert.Empty(outcome.Warnings);
        Assert.Equal(setup.Request, setup.Port.Request);
        Assert.Equal(PilotUpdateSetup.NewScale, outcome.PostRead.InitialState!.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryWriteUnitReceivesTheTextOfThePlanForItsKeyInOrdinalOrder()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        var plan = setup.ExpectedPlan();

        setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2", "C3" }, setup.Port.Written.Select(w => w.Key).ToArray());
        foreach (var (key, text) in setup.Port.Written) Assert.Equal(plan.Envelope(key), text);
        Assert.Equal(plan, setup.Port.Plan);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheDraftIsRebasedAndCleanFromTheRereadOfPostWhenTheRunIsClosed()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        var plan = setup.ExpectedPlan();
        // The text of the origin changes with the write: POST finds the new text, and the origin of POST is the KEY of the plan.
        Assert.NotEqual(setup.Scan.RawEnvelopes["A1"], plan.Envelope("A1"));
        var outcome = setup.Run();

        var closed = setup.Draft.CompleteApply(outcome);

        Assert.True(closed.Accepted);
        Assert.True(closed.Rebased);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, setup.Draft.Status);
        Assert.False(setup.Draft.IsDirty);
        Assert.Equal(PilotUpdateSetup.NewScale, setup.Draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale);
        Assert.Equal(plan.Envelope("A1"), setup.Draft.StaleBase.Entries["A1"].EnvelopeText);
        Assert.Equal(plan.Envelope("A1"), setup.Draft.InitialDraftState.Origin);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ PREPARE: the origin, the plan, the survivors and the layers

    [Fact]
    public void ADocumentThatIsNotTheAuthorizedOneStopsTheRunBeforeAnythingIsImported()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.DocumentMatches = false;

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.DocumentMismatchReason, outcome.Cause);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Begin", "ReadForPrepare", "Dispose:scope" }, setup.Port.Calls.ToArray());
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThereIsNoOriginWhenNoMutableMemberHasTheTextOfTheOriginOfTheDraft()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        // The origin of the draft is the text of A1; the drawing no longer has it in any member.
        var others = setup.Members.Select(m => m.Fact.DefinitionKey == "A1" ? IntentKit.Member("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal("pb-02-modulos")) : m).ToArray();
        setup.Port.PrepareDrawing = others;

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.NoOriginReason, outcome.Cause);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("PrepareUnits", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("BeginMutation", setup.Port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SeveralMembersWithTheTextOfTheOriginAreAPreflightWithTheirOwnReasonAndNotTheMisleadingOneOfTheReader()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        var twin = IntentKit.Member("D4", setup.Scan.RawEnvelopes["A1"]);
        setup.Port.PrepareDrawing = setup.Members.Append(twin).ToArray();

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.SeveralOriginsReason, outcome.Cause);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("PrepareUnits", setup.Port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ADifferenceOfTheBaseInPrepareIsAConflictWithItsDifferencesAndNothingIsImported()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.PrepareDrawing = setup.Members.Select(m => m.Fact.DefinitionKey == "B2" ? IntentKit.Member("B2", PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost(), "pb-02-modulos")) : m).ToArray();

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedConflict, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains(new DraftDifference("B2", DraftDifferenceKind.EnvelopeText), outcome.Differences);
        Assert.Null(outcome.Cause);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("PrepareUnits", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("BeginMutation", setup.Port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AReadThatIsNotAdmittedWithTheBaseOfTheDraftIsNotAppliedAdmission()
    {
        var originText = PrepareKit.Frontal();
        var divergent = PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost(), "pb-02-modulos");
        var members = new[] { IntentKit.Member("A1", originText), IntentKit.Member("B2", divergent) };
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", originText), ("B2", divergent)), originText);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);
        var port = new PilotUpdateScopeDouble(members);
        Assert.True(draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);

        var outcome = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(draft, new PushBackPilotUpdateRequest(100, PrepareKit.Rack), port);

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedAdmission, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(outcome.Cause));
        Assert.DoesNotContain("PrepareUnits", port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AViewThatIsNotAvailableInTheOutgoingSystemIsAPreflightAndNothingIsImported()
    {
        var originText = PrepareKit.Frontal();
        var phantom = PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.MissingPost);
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", originText), ("B2", phantom)), originText);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 2.25);
        var port = new PilotUpdateScopeDouble(IntentKit.Member("A1", originText), IntentKit.Member("B2", phantom));
        Assert.True(draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);

        var outcome = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(draft, new PushBackPilotUpdateRequest(100, PrepareKit.Rack), port);

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.ViewUnavailablePrefix + "B2", outcome.Cause, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("PrepareUnits", port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ACleanDraftIsNothingToApplyAndAConflictDraftIsNotApplicableAndAPendingTextIsPendingTextEvenWithoutTheSlot()
    {
        var clean = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        clean.Draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = clean.Draft.InitialDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale;
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, clean.Draft.Status);
        var nothing = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(clean.Draft, clean.Request, clean.Port);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, nothing.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.NothingToApplyReason, nothing.Cause);

        var pending = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        pending.Draft.SetPendingText("AnnotationScale", "3");
        var typed = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(pending.Draft, pending.Request, pending.Port);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, typed.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PendingTextReason, typed.Cause);

        var conflict = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        conflict.Draft.ConfirmBase(PrepareKit.BaseOf(("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal("pb-02-modulos"))));
        var notApplicable = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(conflict.Draft, conflict.Request, conflict.Port);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, notApplicable.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.NotApplicableReason, notApplicable.Cause);
    }

    [Fact]

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ MUTATE: the re-read is the authority (INV-F4T4B-01 and 02)

    [Fact]
    public void ADifferenceFoundByTheRereadOfMutateIsAConflictAndNoUnitIsWritten()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.RereadDrawing = setup.Members.Select(m => m.Fact.DefinitionKey == "C3" ? IntentKit.Member("C3", PrepareKit.Planta("pb-02-modulos")) : m).ToArray();

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedConflict, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains(new DraftDifference("C3", DraftDifferenceKind.EnvelopeText), outcome.Differences);
        Assert.Empty(setup.Port.Written);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Seal", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.Contains("Dispose:mutation", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Post", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.Equal(setup.Members.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray(), setup.Port.Drawing.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray());
    }

    [Fact]

    [Fact]
    public void TheSurvivorsAndTheLockedLayersAreCheckedAgainInMutateAndNothingIsWritten()
    {
        var noSurvivors = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        noSurvivors.Port.RereadDrawing = noSurvivors.Members.Select(m => new IntentItem(PilotUpdateScopeDouble.FactOf(m.Fact.DefinitionKey, m.Text!, layoutReferences: 0), m.Text)).ToArray();
        var first = noSurvivors.Run();

        Assert.Empty(noSurvivors.Port.Written);

        Assert.Empty(locked.Port.Written);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the table of D-13 by frontier (INV-F4T4B-03)

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("Begin")]
    [InlineData("ReadForPrepare")]
    [InlineData("PrepareUnits")]
    [InlineData("BeginMutation")]
    [InlineData("Reread")]
    [InlineData("WriteUnit:A1")]
    [InlineData("WriteUnit:B2")]
    public void AnExceptionBeforeTheSealIsNotAppliedFailureWithItsCauseAndTheSealIsNeverCalled(string call)
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt[call] = Boom("boom at " + call);

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("boom at " + call, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Seal", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Post", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.Equal(setup.Members.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray(), setup.Port.Drawing.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray());
        // Whatever scope was opened was released.
        Assert.Equal(call == "Begin" ? 0 : 1, setup.Port.Calls.Count(c => c == "Dispose:scope"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AbortThatFailsAfterAUnitStartedIsCommitThrewWithAbortFailedAndNoRollbackIsClaimed()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["WriteUnit:B2"] = Boom("unit boom");
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("abort boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.AbortFailedPrefix, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("abort boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Seal", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Post", setup.Port.Calls);

        var closed = setup.Draft.CompleteApply(outcome);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Unknown, setup.Draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(DraftUnknownOrigin.CommitThrew, setup.Draft.UnknownOrigin);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, closed.Kind);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AReleaseThatFailsWithoutSealAndWithoutAUnitStartedIsNotAppliedFailureAndNotAnUncertainOutcome()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Reread"] = Boom("reread boom");
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("abort boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("reread boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("abort boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Empty(setup.Port.Written);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ASealThatThrowsIsCommitThrewAndPostIsNeverReached()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Seal"] = Boom("seal boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.SealFailedPrefix, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("seal boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.DoesNotContain("Post", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.Contains("Dispose:mutation", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.Contains("Dispose:scope", setup.Port.Calls);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ASealThatReturnsAndAReleaseThatThrowsGoesToPostAndTheReleaseIsAWarning()

        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("release boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Contains(outcome.Warnings, w => w.Contains("release boom", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains("Post", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.True(outcome.PostRead!.IsAdmitted);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void APostThatThrowsIsAppliedPostRereadFailedWithTheWarningsThatWereKnown()

    [Fact]
    public void APostWithoutValuesOrWithoutItsRereadIsAppliedPostRereadFailed()
    {
        var withoutValues = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        withoutValues.Port.NullPostValues = true;
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedPostRereadFailed, withoutValues.Run().Kind);

        var withoutScan = new PilotUpdateSetup();

        var outcome = withoutScan.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedPostRereadFailed, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostRereadMissingReason, outcome.Cause);
    }

    [Fact]

    {

        Assert.Equal(DraftUnknownOrigin.PostReread, setup.Draft.UnknownOrigin);

    }

    [Fact]
    public void AComparisonThatThrowsAfterTheSealIsAppliedPostRereadFailed()
    {

        // The scan of POST carries a null fact: building the read of POST throws after the seal.
        setup.Port.PostOverride = request => new PushBackPilotPostValues(
            false,
            new PushBackPilotScanValues(true, new RackSiblingScanFact[] { null! }, new Dictionary<string, string>()),
            new Dictionary<string, string>(),
            null);

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedPostRereadFailed, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostFailurePrefix, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the release of the outer scope: it never changes what was decided

    [Fact]
    public void AnOuterReleaseThatThrowsAfterPostTurnsAppliedIntoAppliedWithWarningsAndKeepsTheOtherWarnings()
    {
        var plain = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        plain.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var outcome = plain.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedWithWarnings, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains(outcome.Warnings, w => w.Contains("outer boom", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        var warned = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        warned.Port.MissingPieces["C3"] = new[] { "PIEZA_Z" };
        warned.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var second = warned.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedWithWarnings, second.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(2, second.Warnings.Count);
        Assert.True(second.PostRead!.IsAdmitted);

        // Both end Clean: the warnings do not change the status of the draft.
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, StatusAfter(plain, outcome));
    }

    private static DraftStatus StatusAfter(PilotUpdateSetup setup, DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState> outcome)
    {
        setup.Draft.CompleteApply(outcome);
        return setup.Draft.Status;
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnOuterReleaseThatThrowsOutsidePostAddsItsCauseToTheClassificationThatWasAlreadyDecided()
    {
        var preflight = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        preflight.Port.DocumentMatches = false;
        preflight.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var first = preflight.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, first.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.DocumentMismatchReason, Cause(first), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("outer boom", Cause(first), StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var failure = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        failure.Port.ThrowAt["PrepareUnits"] = Boom("import boom");
        failure.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var second = failure.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, second.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("import boom", Cause(second), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("outer boom", Cause(second), StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var committed = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        committed.Port.ThrowAt["Seal"] = Boom("seal boom");
        committed.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var third = committed.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, third.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("outer boom", Cause(third), StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var abort = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        abort.Port.ThrowAt["WriteUnit:A1"] = Boom("unit boom");
        abort.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("abort boom");
        abort.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");
        var fourth = abort.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, fourth.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("abort boom", Cause(fourth), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("outer boom", Cause(fourth), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AnOuterReleaseThatThrowsOnAConflictIsDroppedBecauseAConflictCarriesNoCause()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.RereadDrawing = setup.Members.Select(m => m.Fact.DefinitionKey == "C3" ? IntentKit.Member("C3", PrepareKit.Planta("pb-02-modulos")) : m).ToArray();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:scope"] = Boom("outer boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        // The declared limitation of the package: the exception of the release is lost, the classification is not.
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedConflict, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Null(outcome.Cause);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the precedence between the rows

    [Fact]
    public void AConflictOrAPreflightOfMutateStaysWhatItIsEvenWhenTheReleaseOfTheScopeOfMutateThrows()
    {
        var conflict = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        conflict.Port.RereadDrawing = conflict.Members.Select(m => m.Fact.DefinitionKey == "C3" ? IntentKit.Member("C3", PrepareKit.Planta("pb-02-modulos")) : m).ToArray();
        conflict.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("release boom");
        var first = conflict.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedConflict, first.Kind);
        Assert.NotEmpty(first.Differences);

        var preflight = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        preflight.Port.LockedAtReread = new[] { "B2" };
        preflight.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("release boom");
        var second = preflight.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedPreflight, second.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.LockedLayerPrefix + "B2", Cause(second), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("release boom", Cause(second), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Empty(preflight.Port.Written);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ASealThatThrewKeepsBeingCommitThrewBecauseOfTheSealEvenIfTheAbortThrowsToo()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Seal"] = Boom("seal boom");
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["Dispose:mutation"] = Boom("abort boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.CommitThrew, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.SealFailedPrefix, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("abort boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AbortFailedOnlyAppliesToANotAppliedFailureWithAUnitStarted()
    {
        // A preflight of MUTATE has no unit started: it stays a preflight (the test above); a unit that threw and a clean abort stays NotAppliedFailure.
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.ThrowAt["WriteUnit:C3"] = Boom("unit boom");

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Contains("unit boom", Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2" }, setup.Port.Written.Select(w => w.Key).ToArray());
        Assert.Contains("Dispose:mutation", setup.Port.Calls);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.AbortFailedPrefix, Cause(outcome), StringComparison.Ordinal);
        // The two units that were staged before the failure are not in the drawing: the abort threw them away.
        Assert.Equal(setup.Members.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray(), setup.Port.Drawing.Select(m => m.Text).ToArray());
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ the run is total (INV-F4T4B-03)

    [Fact]
    public void TheRunNeverThrowsForMissingArgumentsOrForAPortThatAnswersNothing()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(null!, setup.Request, setup.Port).Kind);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(setup.Draft, null!, setup.Port).Kind);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(setup.Draft, setup.Request, null!).Kind);

        var noScope = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        noScope.Port.ReturnNullScope = true;
        var outcome = noScope.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.NoScopeReason, outcome.Cause);

        var noStart = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        noStart.Port.NullMutationStart = true;
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.NotAppliedFailure, noStart.Run().Kind);
        Assert.Contains("Dispose:scope", noStart.Port.Calls);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ POST: the names, the warnings and the comparison

    private static PushBackPilotIntentScan NamedScan(string name) => PrepareKit.Scan(
        IntentKit.Member("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal(name: name)),
        IntentKit.Member("B2", PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost(), name: name)),
        IntentKit.Member("C3", PrepareKit.Planta(name: name)));

    [Fact]
    public void TheDesiredNamesFollowTheRuleOfTheClassicEditorPerView()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup(() => NamedScan("  Mi rack  "));
        var post = PrepareKit.ExistingPost();

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.True(outcome.Kind == DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Cause);
        var names = setup.Port.PostRequest!.DesiredNames;
        Assert.Equal(3, names.Count);
        Assert.Equal("Mi rack - frontal entrada-salida", names["A1"]);
        Assert.Equal("Mi rack - lateral " + (post + 1), names["B2"]);
        Assert.Equal("Mi rack - planta", names["C3"]);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AViewWithABlankNameHasNoDesiredNameAndGivesNoWarningEvenIfItsDefinitionHasAnotherName()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup(() => NamedScan("   "));

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.True(outcome.Kind == DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Cause);
        Assert.Empty(setup.Port.PostRequest!.DesiredNames);
        Assert.Empty(outcome.Warnings);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ANameThatDiffersAfterTheRenameIsAWarningAndACaseOnlyDifferenceIsNot()
    {
        var differs = new PilotUpdateSetup(() => NamedScan("Mi rack"));
        differs.Port.NameAfterPost = (key, desired) => key == "C3" ? "Ocupado" : desired;
        var outcome = differs.Run();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedWithWarnings, outcome.Kind);
        var warning = Assert.Single(outcome.Warnings);
        Assert.Contains("C3", warning, StringComparison.Ordinal);

        var sameCase = new PilotUpdateSetup(() => NamedScan("Mi rack"));
        sameCase.Port.NameAfterPost = (key, desired) => desired.ToUpperInvariant();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, sameCase.Run().Kind);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheMissingPiecesAndAFailedRegenerationAreWarningsAndTheStateStaysClean()
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.MissingPieces["A1"] = new[] { "POSTE_Q", "PLACA_R" };
        setup.Port.RegenFailure = "InvalidOperationException: regen";

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedWithWarnings, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(3, outcome.Warnings.Count);
        Assert.Contains(outcome.Warnings, w => w.Contains("POSTE_Q", StringComparison.Ordinal) && w.Contains("A1", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains(outcome.Warnings, w => w.Contains("regen", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        var closed = setup.Draft.CompleteApply(outcome);
        Assert.True(closed.Rebased);
        Assert.Equal(DraftStatus.Clean, setup.Draft.Status);
        Assert.Equal(outcome.Warnings.ToArray(), setup.Draft.Warnings.ToArray());
    }

    // The comparison of POST is byte for byte: nothing is normalized, so each of these differences fails it.
    [Theory]
    [InlineData("trailing")]
    [InlineData("crlf")]
    [InlineData("case")]
    public void ThePostComparisonDoesNotNormalizeAnything(string change)
    {
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        setup.Port.TextAfterSeal = (key, text) =>
        {
            if (key != "B2") return text;
            return change switch
            {
                "trailing" => text + " ",
                "crlf" => text.Replace("\n", "\r\n", StringComparison.Ordinal) + "\r\n",
                _ => text.Replace("\"lateral\"", "\"Lateral\"", StringComparison.Ordinal),
            };
        };

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.NotEqual(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.NotEqual(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedWithWarnings, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.AppliedPostRereadFailed, outcome.Kind);

        // M-06 (b): the cause names the envelope that differs (B2), not just any cause.
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostEnvelopeDiffersPrefix + "B2", outcome.Cause, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePostComparisonFailsForAnotherSetOfKeysAnotherEnvelopeAndAnotherScale()
    {
        var plan = new PilotUpdateSetup().ExpectedPlan();
        var good = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", plan.Members.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.EnvelopeText)).ToArray()));
        Assert.Null(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, good));

        var fewer = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", plan.Members.Take(2).Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.EnvelopeText)).ToArray()));
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostKeysDifferReason, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, fewer));

        var more = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", plan.Members.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.EnvelopeText)).Append(IntentKit.Member("D4", plan.Envelope("C3")!)).ToArray()));
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostKeysDifferReason, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, more));

        var edited = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", plan.Members.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.Key == "C3" ? m.EnvelopeText + " " : m.EnvelopeText)).ToArray()));
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostEnvelopeDiffersPrefix + "C3", PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, edited), StringComparison.Ordinal);

        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostRereadMissingReason, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, null));
        var notAdmitted = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "ZZ", IntentKit.Member("A1", plan.Envelope("A1")!)));
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostNotAdmittedPrefix, PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, notAdmitted), StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePostComparisonSeesAnotherScaleInTheStateThatTheRereadMaterializes()
    {
        // The plan of another scale, over the very same envelopes of the drawing: the envelopes of the plan carry the scale of the design, so a
        // re-read of the envelopes of a plan of another scale does not match it.
        var plan = new PilotUpdateSetup().ExpectedPlan();
        var other = new PilotUpdateSetup(scale: 3.5).ExpectedPlan();
        var read = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", other.Members.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.EnvelopeText)).ToArray()));

        var cause = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, read);
        Assert.NotNull(cause);

        // M-06 (c): the branch of the scale (PostScaleDiffersReason) is UNREACHABLE with equal envelopes: the scale that the re-read
        // materializes comes from the design inside the envelope, so envelopes that are equal byte for byte give the scale of the plan. A
        // difference of scale always shows first as a difference of envelope, and that is what is fixed here.
        Assert.StartsWith(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostEnvelopeDiffersPrefix, cause, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.NotEqual(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.PostScaleDiffersReason, cause);
        var same = IntentKit.Read(IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", plan.Members.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.EnvelopeText)).ToArray()));
        Assert.Null(PushBackPilotUpdateRun.ComparePost(plan, same));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ThePostRereadUsesTheKeyOfTheOriginOfThePlanAndNotTheOneOfTheTextThatChanged()
    {
        // The origin of the draft was the text of A1; after the write A1 has the new text, which no member of the drawing had before.
        var setup = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        var plan = setup.ExpectedPlan();
        Assert.Equal("A1", plan.OriginKey);

        var outcome = setup.Run();

        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Kind);
        Assert.Equal(plan.Envelope("A1"), setup.Port.Drawing.Single(m => m.Key == "A1").Text);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheRunKeepsNoTextOfTheDrawingBetweenTwoRuns()
    {
        // M-06 (a): two runs over the SAME double, the second one from a draft read from what the first one left in the drawing and with
        // another scale. Nothing of the texts of the first run may survive in the second.
        var first = new PilotUpdateSetup();
        Assert.Equal(DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, first.Run().Kind);
        var firstWritten = first.Port.Written.ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(firstWritten);

        var afterFirst = IntentKit.Scan(PrepareKit.Rack, "A1", first.Port.Drawing.Select(m => IntentKit.Member(m.Key, m.Text)).ToArray());
        var draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(IntentKit.Read(afterFirst));
        PrepareKit.SetScale(draft, 3.5);
        Assert.True(draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);

        var outcome = PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(draft, first.Request, first.Port);

        Assert.True(outcome.Kind == DraftApplyOutcomeKind.Applied, outcome.Cause);
        var secondWritten = first.Port.Written.Skip(firstWritten.Count).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(firstWritten.Select(w => w.Key).ToArray(), secondWritten.Select(w => w.Key).ToArray());
        Assert.All(secondWritten, w => Assert.DoesNotContain(firstWritten, f => f.Text == w.Text));
        Assert.All(secondWritten, w => Assert.Contains("3.5", w.Text, StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }

    // The system of the outgoing design, rebuilt apart from the text of a member of the plan with the same resolver as PREPARE.
    [Fact]
    public void TheSystemIsRebuiltFromTheDesignTextOfAMemberOfThePlan()
    {
        var plan = new PilotUpdateSetup().ExpectedPlan();

        var system = PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.Build(plan.Members[0].DesignText, PilotKit.Catalog);

        Assert.NotNull(system);
        Assert.False(PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.IsComposite(system));
        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.Build("not a design", PilotKit.Catalog));
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.Build(null!, PilotKit.Catalog));
        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.IsComposite(null!));
    }

    // I-64 F4-T4-HOST-C1 (D-C1-01, INV-C1-01, test 1 of D-C1-09): the system that the writer redraws carries the rack name of the envelope of ITS
    // member, raw, because the label of the name of the views is drawn only from it. Everything runs over the text kits of the plan: the members
    // are only deserialized, nothing is drawn.
    [Fact]
    public void TheSystemRebuiltForAMemberCarriesTheRackNameOfItsEnvelope()
    {
        var store = new RackEmbedStore();

        // Every member of the plan of a rack carries the name of its envelope, and nothing else of the system changes. (The reader of the pilot
        // admits a rack whose views share one name, so the three views carry the same one here; different names come below, by hand.)
        var scan = PrepareKit.Scan(
            IntentKit.Member("A1", PrepareKit.Frontal(name: "PB G")),
            IntentKit.Member("B2", PrepareKit.Lateral(PrepareKit.ExistingPost(), name: "PB G")),
            IntentKit.Member("C3", PrepareKit.Planta(name: "PB G")));
        var plan = new PilotUpdateSetup(() => scan).ExpectedPlan();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "B2", "C3" }, plan.Members.Select(m => m.Key).OrderBy(k => k, StringComparer.Ordinal).ToArray());
        foreach (var member in plan.Members)
        {
            var system = PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(member, PilotKit.Catalog);
            var plain = PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.Build(member.DesignText, PilotKit.Catalog);

            Assert.Equal(store.Deserialize(member.EnvelopeText)!.Name, system.Name);
            Assert.Equal("PB G", system.Name);
            Assert.Equal(plain.IsComposite, system.IsComposite);
            Assert.Equal(plain.TotalLength, system.TotalLength);
            Assert.Equal(plain.Fronts!.Count, system.Fronts!.Count);
            Assert.Equal(plain.InOutBeamCatalogId, system.InOutBeamCatalogId);
            Assert.Equal(plain.HighEndBeamCatalogId, system.HighEndBeamCatalogId);
            Assert.Equal(plain.DefensePieceId, system.DefensePieceId);
        }

        // The name goes as it is written: with spaces around it, identical; a blank one, or none, stays blank and draws no label.
        var design = store.Deserialize(PrepareKit.Frontal())!.Design;
        PushBackPilotMemberPlan Member(string text) => new("K0", RackEmbedDocument.ViewFrontal, 0, design!, text);
        string? NameOf(string? written) =>
            PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(Member(IntentKit.Rewrite(PrepareKit.Frontal(), e => e.Name = written)), PilotKit.Catalog).Name;

        // Each member carries the name of its OWN envelope, also when the views of a drawing were named differently.
        Assert.Equal("Rack uno", NameOf("Rack uno"));
        Assert.Equal("Rack dos", NameOf("Rack dos"));
        Assert.Equal("  Mi rack  ", NameOf("  Mi rack  "));
        Assert.Equal("   ", NameOf("   "));
        Assert.True(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NameOf("   ")));
        Assert.True(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NameOf(string.Empty)));
        Assert.True(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(NameOf(null)));

        // The arguments and the envelope that cannot be read: the same exceptions as the help that it extends, and one reason of its own.
        var noMember = Record.Exception(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(null!, PilotKit.Catalog));
        Assert.IsType<ArgumentNullException>(noMember);
        var noCatalog = Record.Exception(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(Member(PrepareKit.Frontal()), null!));
        Assert.IsType<ArgumentNullException>(noCatalog);
        var notADesign = Record.Exception(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(new PushBackPilotMemberPlan("K0", RackEmbedDocument.ViewFrontal, 0, "not a design", PrepareKit.Frontal()), PilotKit.Catalog));
        Assert.IsType<InvalidOperationException>(notADesign);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.UnreadableDesignReason, notADesign!.Message);
        var unreadable = Record.Exception(() => PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.BuildForMember(Member("{ not an envelope"), PilotKit.Catalog));
        Assert.IsType<InvalidOperationException>(unreadable);
        Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotSystemRebuild.UnreadableEnvelopeReason, unreadable!.Message);
    }

/// <summary>I-64 F4-T4B: the standard rack of the tests of the update, a draft with an edited scale and its double.</summary>
internal sealed class PilotUpdateSetup
{
    public const double NewScale = 2.25;

    public PilotUpdateSetup(Func<PushBackPilotIntentScan>? scan = null, double scale = NewScale)
    {
        Scan = (scan ?? (() => PrepareKit.RealScan()))();
        var read = IntentKit.Read(Scan);
        Assert.True(read.Read.IsAdmitted);
        Draft = PrepareKit.DraftOf(read);
        PrepareKit.SetScale(Draft, scale);
        Members = Scan.Facts.Select(f => new IntentItem(f, Scan.RawEnvelopes[f.DefinitionKey])).ToArray();
        Port = new PilotUpdateScopeDouble(Members);
        Request = new PushBackPilotUpdateRequest(100, PrepareKit.Rack);
    }

    public PushBackPilotIntentScan Scan { get; }

    public DraftSession<PushBackPilotDraftState> Draft { get; }

    public IntentItem[] Members { get; }

    public PilotUpdateScopeDouble Port { get; }

    public PushBackPilotUpdateRequest Request { get; }

    /// <summary>The plan that the run builds for the standard rack, computed apart with the pure PREPARE.</summary>
    public PushBackPilotUpdatePlan ExpectedPlan()
    {
        var result = PushBackPilotUpdatePlanner.Prepare(PushBackPilotPrepareInput.From(Draft), IntentKit.Read(Scan), PilotKit.Catalog);
        Assert.True(result.Kind == PushBackPilotPrepareKind.Ready, result.Reason);
        return result.Plan!;
    }

    /// <summary>Takes the slot like the host does, and runs.</summary>
    public DraftApplyOutcome<PushBackPilotDraftState> Run()
    {
        Assert.True(Draft.TryBeginApply().IsStarted);
        return PushBackPilotUpdateRun.Execute(Draft, Request, Port);
    }
}

@@@@ EDIT tests/RackCad.UI.Tests/PushBackDeclaredCorrectionSupport.cs
@@@@ OLD
        /// <summary>PilotoEscribe(t, catalog): lo que el piloto escribe, copia de WLC:84-91 sin referenciarla.</summary>
        internal static string PilotoEscribe(string t, RackCatalog catalog)
        {
            var materialized = PushBackPilotMaterializer.Materialize(t, catalog);
            Assert.Equal(PushBackPilotMaterializationKind.Admitted, materialized.Kind);
            var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(materialized.State, materialized.State, catalog);
            Assert.True(composition.IsComposed, composition.Reason);
            return Persist(new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(catalog).BuildDesign(composition.Editor, composition.Inputs));
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// La linea <c>&lt;nombre&gt; admitida=&lt;b&gt; delta=[…]</c> de una fila: la ventana carga el sobre, y su diseño se
        /// compara con lo que escribe el piloto. La ventana se crea, se carga y se cierra aqui, con la carga dentro del <c>try</c>.
        /// </summary>
        internal static string Paridad(string nombre, string t)
        {
            var w = Ventana();
            try
            {
                Cargar(w, t);
                var admitida = w.CurrentInputsAreValid && w.LastComputation != null && w.LastComputation.IsValid;
                var delta = admitida
                    ? Delta(Persist(w.LastComputation.Design), PilotoEscribe(t, w.Session.Catalog))
                    : new List<string> { "NO-ADMITIDA: " + StatusText(w) };
                return nombre + " admitida=" + admitida + " delta=[" + string.Join(" ;; ", delta) + "]";
            }
            finally { Cerrar(w); }
        }

        private static readonly string[] Texts =
        {
            "", " ", "   ", "\t", "abc", "1x", "x1", "1.5", "1,5", "1.50", "1,50", " 2 ", "+3", "-3", "-0.5", "0", "0.0", "0,0", "-0",
            "0.001", "0,001", "1000000", "1e3", "1E-2", "1e999", "NaN", "Infinity", "-Infinity", "∞", "1.2.3", "1,2,3", "1 000", "1.000,5",
            "1,000.5", "١٢", "12٫5", ".5", ",5", "5.", "5,", "--1", "+-1", "0x10", "1_0", "1/2", "٣", "１２",

        public static IEnumerable<object[]> Cultures() =>
            new[] { "es-MX", "en-US", "de-DE", string.Empty }.Select(name => new object[] { name });

        private static T InCulture<T>(string name, Func<T> body)
        {
            var previous = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
            try
            {
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture = CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo(name);
                return body();
            }
            finally
            {
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture = previous;
            }
        }

        [Theory]
        [MemberData(nameof(Cultures))]
        public void TheAdapterGivesTheSameVerdictAndMessageAsTheClassicBoxInEveryCulture(string culture)
        {
            var adapter = new PushBackPilotFieldAdapter();

            foreach (var text in Texts)
            {
                var classic = InCulture(culture, () => NumericFieldValidation.Validate(text, false, false, 0, null, minimumInclusive: false));
                var pilot = InCulture(culture, () => adapter.Validate(PushBackPilotFieldAdapter.AnnotationScaleKey, text));

                Assert.True(classic.IsValid == pilot.IsValid, $"[{culture}] '{text}': classic={classic.IsValid} pilot={pilot.IsValid}");
                Assert.True(classic.Message == pilot.Message, $"[{culture}] '{text}': classic='{classic.Message}' pilot='{pilot.Message}'");
            }
        }

        [Theory]
        [MemberData(nameof(Cultures))]
        public void AValidTextAppliesTheSameNumberTheClassicBoxParses(string culture)
        {
            foreach (var text in Texts)
            {
                var classic = InCulture(culture, () => NumericFieldValidation.Validate(text, false, false, 0, null, minimumInclusive: false));
                if (!classic.IsValid) continue;

                var applied = InCulture(culture, () =>
                {
                    var state = new PushBackPilotDraftState(new PushBackCompositeEditorState(), PushBackEditorInputs.NewDesign(), "origin");
                    new PushBackPilotFieldAdapter().Apply(state, new Dictionary<string, string> { [PushBackPilotFieldAdapter.AnnotationScaleKey] = text });
                    return state.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale;
                });

                Assert.Equal(classic.Value.Value, applied);
            }
        }

        private static string Persist(RackCad.Domain.Systems.PushBack.PushBackDesign design) =>
            new RackProjectStore().Serialize(RackProject.ForPushBack(design)).Replace("\r\n", "\n");

        /// <summary>What the classic window ends up with after LoadExisting; Design is null when it rejects the values.</summary>
        private sealed class WindowResult
        {
            public string Design;
            public RackCatalog Catalog;
        }

        private static WindowResult LoadInWindow(string text)
        {
            return StaTestRunner.Run(() =>
            {
                var embed = new RackEmbedStore().Deserialize(text);
                var project = new RackProjectStore().Deserialize(embed.Design);
                var window = new RackPushBackSystemWindow(canInsertInAutoCad: true);
                window.LoadExisting(project.PushBackDesign, embed.Id, embed.Name, project);
                var result = new WindowResult { Catalog = window.Session.Catalog };
                if (window.CurrentInputsAreValid && window.LastComputation != null && window.LastComputation.IsValid)
                {
                    result.Design = Persist(window.LastComputation.Design);
                }

                return result;
            });
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// What the pilot writes after Keep-mine (V2 changed content the pilot does not edit; the draft changed the scale) opens in
        /// the classic window with the content of V2 and the scale of the draft: nothing of V2 is reverted on the way.
        /// </summary>
        [Fact]
        public void TheDocumentThePilotWritesAfterKeepMineOpensInTheClassicWindowWithTheNewBaseAndTheScaleOfTheDraft()
        {
            var v1 = Fixture("pb-01-base");
            var catalog = LoadInWindow(v1).Catalog;

            // V2: the same rack with another pallet tolerance, assembled by the product itself.
            var changed = PushBackPilotMaterializer.Materialize(v1, catalog).State;
            changed.Inputs.PalletTolerance += 2.0;
            var store = new RackEmbedStore();
            var embed = store.Deserialize(v1);
            embed.Design = new RackProjectStore().Serialize(RackProject.ForPushBack(
                new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(catalog).BuildDesign(changed.Editor, changed.Inputs)));
            var v2 = store.Serialize(embed);
            var v1Tolerance = DesignNumber(LoadInWindow(v1).Design, "PalletTolerance");
            var v2Tolerance = DesignNumber(LoadInWindow(v2).Design, "PalletTolerance");
            Assert.NotEqual(v1Tolerance, v2Tolerance);

            // The draft opens V1, edits the scale and keeps its edits on V2.
            var draft = new DraftRegistry().Begin(
                new SessionId(1), "R1",
                DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(
                    DraftStaleBase.Create(new[] { new DraftStaleBaseEntry("D1", DraftMembership.Mutable, "frontal", "-1", v1) }),
                    PushBackPilotMaterializer.Materialize(v1, catalog).State),
                PushBackPilotStateComparer.Instance, PushBackPilotStateComparer.Copy).Draft;
            draft.CurrentDraftState.Inputs.Annotations.AnnotationScale = 2.25;
            var second = PushBackPilotMaterializer.Materialize(v2, catalog).State;
            var keep = draft.KeepMineOnCurrentVersion(
                DraftIntentRead<PushBackPilotDraftState>.Admitted(
                    DraftStaleBase.Create(new[] { new DraftStaleBaseEntry("D1", DraftMembership.Mutable, "frontal", "-1", v2) }), second),
                confirmed: true);
            Assert.Equal(DraftReloadStatus.Reloaded, keep.Status);
            draft.SetCurrentDraftState(PushBackPilotMaterializer.KeepPilotEditsOn(second, draft.CurrentDraftState));

            var composition = PushBackPilotMaterializer.ComposeForWrite(draft.InitialDraftState, draft.CurrentDraftState, catalog);
            Assert.True(composition.IsComposed, composition.Reason);
            embed.Design = new RackProjectStore().Serialize(RackProject.ForPushBack(
                new PushBackCompositeEditorAssembler(catalog).BuildDesign(composition.Editor, composition.Inputs)));
            var opened = LoadInWindow(store.Serialize(embed));

            Assert.NotNull(opened.Design);
            Assert.Equal(2.25, DesignNumber(opened.Design, "AnnotationScale"));
            Assert.Equal(v2Tolerance, DesignNumber(opened.Design, "PalletTolerance"));
        }

        private static double DesignNumber(string design, string structureProperty)
        {
            using (var document = JsonDocument.Parse(design))
            {
                return document.RootElement.GetProperty("PushBack").GetProperty("Structure").GetProperty(structureProperty).GetDouble();
            }
        }
